* Start of comment: CHG-13
*  CLEAR eban.
*  SELECT SINGLE * FROM eban
*  WHERE banfn IN s_banfn
*    AND loekz = ''.
*  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
*    MESSAGE e000(zcu) WITH 'ไม่พบข้อมูลใบขอเสนอซื้อ'.
*  ENDIF.
* End of comment: CHG-13

  CLEAR eban.
*<<< BOE CHG-25
*  SELECT SINGLE * FROM eban
*  WHERE banfn IN s_banfn
*    AND loekz = space.

  SELECT * FROM eban
    INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_eban_temp)
  WHERE banfn IN @s_banfn
    AND loekz = @space.

  SORT lt_eban_temp BY bnfpo.
  READ TABLE lt_eban_temp INTO eban INDEX 1.

  v_matkl = eban-matkl.
  v_ekgrp = eban-ekgrp.

  SELECT SINGLE *
    FROM ebkn
    INTO @DATA(lw_ebkn_temp)
  WHERE banfn IN @s_banfn
    AND loekz = @space.

  IF eban-geber IS INITIAL.
    eban-geber = lw_ebkn_temp-geber.
  ENDIF.
  IF eban-fistl IS INITIAL.
    eban-fistl = lw_ebkn_temp-fistl.
  ENDIF.
*>>> EOE CHG-25


*<< หาก PR มี Material Group = 002XXXXX ให้ Default : "ขอจ้าง"
*หาก PR มี Material Group = 002XXXX7 ให้ Default : "ขอเช่า"
*อื่นๆ Default : "ขอซื้อ"
  CLEAR: v_r1,r1,v_r2,r2,v_r3,r3.
  IF v_matkl+0(3) = '002'.
    IF v_matkl+7(1) = '7'.
      v_r3 = 'X'.
      r3 = 'X'.
    ELSE.
      v_r2 = 'X'.
      r2 = 'X'.
    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    v_r1 = 'X'.
    r1 = 'X'.
  ENDIF.

*<<< BOI CHG-25

  REFRESH lt_frgzu.
  SELECT DISTINCT banfn, bnfpo, frgzu, ekgrp
    FROM eban
    INTO TABLE @lt_frgzu
*      WHERE banfn = @eban-banfn
    WHERE banfn IN @s_banfn
      AND loekz = ' '.
*  ENDSELECT.

  IF lt_frgzu[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SORT lt_frgzu BY banfn bnfpo ASCENDING.
    LOOP AT lt_frgzu INTO DATA(lw_frgzu).
      LOOP AT lt_frgzu INTO DATA(lw_frgzu_tmp) WHERE banfn = lw_frgzu-banfn
                                                 AND bnfpo <> lw_frgzu-bnfpo.
        DELETE lt_frgzu WHERE banfn = lw_frgzu_tmp-banfn
                          AND bnfpo = lw_frgzu_tmp-bnfpo.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.

    READ TABLE lt_frgzu INTO DATA(lw_frgzu_index1) INDEX 1.
    LOOP AT lt_frgzu INTO lw_frgzu WHERE frgzu <> lw_frgzu_index1-frgzu
                                      OR ekgrp <> lw_frgzu_index1-ekgrp.
      MESSAGE 'ข้อมูลผู้ลงนามไม่ตรงกัน ไม่สามารถพิมพ์เอกสารพร้อมกันได้' TYPE 'E'.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

*      FROM ztwf_pr_log
*      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_ztwf_pr_log)
*        WHERE banfn = @eban-banfn
*          AND frgco = '10'.
*
*      IF sy-subrc = 0.
*
*        SORT lt_ztwf_pr_log BY crdate DESCENDING crtime DESCENDING.
*        READ TABLE lt_ztwf_pr_log INTO DATA(lw_ztwf_pr_log) INDEX 1.
*        IF lw_ztwf_pr_log-us_name IS NOT INITIAL.
*          SELECT pernr, fistl, geber, xubname, ekgrp
*          FROM ztmm_rel_user
*          INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_pernr)
*          WHERE xubname = @lw_ztwf_pr_log-cruser
*            AND frgco = '10'
*            AND ( fistl = @eban-fistl
*               OR fistl = '*' )
*            AND ( geber = @eban-geber
*               OR geber = '*' ).
*        ELSE.
*          SELECT pernr, fistl, geber, xubname, ekgrp
*          FROM ztmm_rel_user
*          INTO TABLE @lt_pernr
*          WHERE xubname = @lw_ztwf_pr_log-cruser
*            AND endda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
*            AND begda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
*            AND frgco = '10'
*            AND ( fistl = @eban-fistl
*               OR fistl = '*' )
*            AND ( geber = @eban-geber
*               OR geber = '*' ).
*        ENDIF.
*
*        SORT lt_pernr BY fistl DESCENDING geber DESCENDING.
*        CLEAR: v_flag_name.
*        LOOP AT lt_pernr INTO DATA(lw_pernr).
*          IF eban-ekgrp CP lw_pernr-ekgrp AND eban-fistl CP lw_pernr-fistl AND eban-geber CP lw_pernr-geber AND v_flag_name IS INITIAL.
*            v_flag_name = 'X'.
*            SELECT a~pernr, a~vorna AS vorna_sig, a~nachn AS nachn_sig, a~vorsw AS vorsw_sig, a~anrlt AS anrlt_sig,
*                          b~vorna AS vorna_pa, b~nachn AS nachn_pa, b~vorsw AS vorsw_pa, b~titl2 AS titl2_pa,
*                          esign, stext, tline, c~subty,
*                          c~plans AS plans_9001,
*                          d~plans AS plans_0001,
*                          e~anrlt AS anrlt_t522t
*            INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_sig_name)
*            FROM ztbc_signature AS a LEFT JOIN pa0002 AS b ON a~pernr = b~pernr
*                                                          AND b~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
*                                                          AND b~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
*                                     LEFT JOIN pa9001 AS c ON a~pernr = c~pernr
*                                                          AND c~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
*                                                          AND c~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
*                                                          AND ( c~subty = '0001'
*                                                             OR c~subty = '0002'
*                                                             OR c~subty = '0003' )
*                                     LEFT JOIN pa0001 AS d ON a~pernr = d~pernr
*                                                          AND d~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
*                                                          AND d~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
*                                     LEFT JOIN t522t  AS e ON b~anred = e~anred
*                                                          AND e~sprsl = '2'
*            WHERE a~pernr = @lw_pernr-pernr
*              AND a~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
*              AND a~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.
*
*            IF sy-subrc = 0.
*              SORT lt_sig_name BY subty.
*              READ TABLE lt_sig_name INTO DATA(lw_sig_name) INDEX 1.
*              p_nam11 = lw_sig_name-pernr.
*              gv_sign_10 = lw_sig_name-esign.
*              REFRESH: lt_sig_name.
*              IF lw_sig_name-plans_9001 IS NOT INITIAL.
*                SELECT SINGLE tline
*                  INTO @lv_hrt1002_tline
*                  FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
*                  WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name-pernr
*                       OR objid = @lw_sig_name-plans_9001 ).
*              ELSEIF lw_sig_name-plans_0001 IS NOT INITIAL.
*                SELECT SINGLE tline
*                  INTO @lv_hrt1002_tline
*                  FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
*                  WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name-pernr
*                       OR objid = @lw_sig_name-plans_0001 ).
*              ENDIF.
*
*              IF lv_hrt1002_tline IS NOT INITIAL.
*                t13_txt2 = lv_hrt1002_tline.
*              ELSE.
*                t13_txt2 = lw_sig_name-tline.
*              ENDIF.
*
*              IF lw_sig_name-vorsw_pa IS NOT INITIAL OR lw_sig_name-titl2_pa IS NOT INITIAL.
*                t13_txt1 = lw_sig_name-vorsw_pa && lw_sig_name-titl2_pa.
*              ELSE.
*                t13_txt3 = lw_sig_name-anrlt_t522t.
*              ENDIF.
*              IF t13_txt1 IS INITIAL OR t13_txt3 IS INITIAL.
*                IF lw_sig_name-vorsw_sig IS NOT INITIAL.
*                  t13_txt1 = lw_sig_name-vorsw_sig.
*                ELSE.
*                  t13_txt3 = lw_sig_name-anrlt_sig.
*                ENDIF.
*              ENDIF.
*
*              IF lw_sig_name-vorna_sig IS NOT INITIAL AND lw_sig_name-nachn_sig IS NOT INITIAL.
*                t13_txt3 = t13_txt3 && lw_sig_name-vorna_sig.
*                t13_txt4 = lw_sig_name-nachn_sig.
*              ELSE.
*                t13_txt3 = t13_txt3 && lw_sig_name-vorna_pa.
*                t13_txt4 = lw_sig_name-nachn_pa.
*              ENDIF.
*            ENDIF.
*          ENDIF.
*        ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.

AT SELECTION-SCREEN ON VALUE-REQUEST FOR p_nam11.

  REFRESH: gt_pa0002, gt_pa0002_disp.

                          gt_pa0002_disp

  CALL FUNCTION 'F4IF_INT_TABLE_VALUE_REQUEST'
    EXPORTING
      retfield        = 'PERNR'
      value_org       = 'S'
*     dynpprog        = sy-repid
*     dynpnr          = sy-dynnr
    TABLES
      value_tab       = gt_pa0002_disp
      return_tab      = gt_ret_tab
*     dynpfld_mapping = lt_map
    EXCEPTIONS
      parameter_error = 1
      no_values_found = 2
      OTHERS          = 3.

  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    CLEAR: t13_txt1, t13_txt2, t13_txt3, t13_txt4.
    READ TABLE gt_ret_tab INTO DATA(lw_ret_tab) INDEX 1.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      p_nam11 = lw_ret_tab-fieldval.
      READ TABLE gt_pa0002 INTO DATA(lw_pa0002) WITH KEY pernr = p_nam11.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.

        SELECT a~pernr, a~vorsw AS vorsw_pa, a~titl2 AS titl2_pa, a~knznm,
                      b~plans AS plans_9001, b~subty,
                      c~plans AS plans_0001,
                      d~anrlt AS anrlt_t522t
              INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_sig_name)
              FROM pa0002 AS a LEFT JOIN pa9001 AS b ON a~pernr = b~pernr
                                                    AND b~begda <= @sy-datum
                                                    AND b~endda >= @sy-datum
                                                    AND ( b~subty = '0001'
                                                       OR b~subty = '0002'
                                                       OR b~subty = '0003' )
                               LEFT JOIN pa0001 AS c ON a~pernr = c~pernr
                                                    AND c~begda <= @sy-datum
                                                    AND c~endda >= @sy-datum
                               LEFT JOIN t522t  AS d ON a~anred = d~anred
                                                    AND d~sprsl = '2'
              WHERE a~pernr = @p_nam11
                AND a~begda <= @sy-datum
                AND a~endda >= @sy-datum.

        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          SORT lt_sig_name BY subty.
          READ TABLE lt_sig_name INTO DATA(lw_sig_name) INDEX 1.
          p_nam11 = lw_sig_name-pernr.
          REFRESH: lt_sig_name.
          IF lw_sig_name-plans_9001 IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE tline
              INTO @DATA(lv_hrt1002_tline)
              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name-plans_9001 ).
          ELSEIF lw_sig_name-plans_0001 IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE tline
              INTO @lv_hrt1002_tline
              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name-plans_0001 ).
          ENDIF.

          IF lv_hrt1002_tline IS NOT INITIAL.
            t13_txt2 = lv_hrt1002_tline.
          ENDIF.

          IF lw_sig_name-knznm = '01'.
            CLEAR: gv_fullname.
            CALL FUNCTION 'ZHR_GET_TITLENAME'
              EXPORTING
                pernr     = p_nam11
              IMPORTING
                full_name = gv_fullname.
            MOVE gv_fullname TO t13_txt1.
          ELSE.
            IF lw_sig_name-vorsw_pa IS NOT INITIAL OR lw_sig_name-titl2_pa IS NOT INITIAL.
              t13_txt1 = lw_sig_name-vorsw_pa && lw_sig_name-titl2_pa.
            ELSE.
              t13_txt3 = lw_sig_name-anrlt_t522t.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

          t13_txt3 = t13_txt3 && lw_pa0002-vorna.
          t13_txt4 = lw_pa0002-nachn.

          CLEAR: gt_dynpfields[],
                 gs_dynpfields.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldname  = 'T13_TXT3'.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldvalue = t13_txt3.
          APPEND gs_dynpfields TO gt_dynpfields.

          CLEAR gs_dynpfields.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldname  = 'T13_TXT4'.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldvalue = t13_txt4.
          APPEND gs_dynpfields TO gt_dynpfields.

          CLEAR: gs_dynpfields.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldname  = 'T13_TXT1'.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldvalue = t13_txt1.
          APPEND gs_dynpfields TO gt_dynpfields.

          CLEAR gs_dynpfields.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldname  = 'T13_TXT2'.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldvalue = t13_txt2.
          APPEND gs_dynpfields TO gt_dynpfields.


          CALL FUNCTION 'DYNP_VALUES_UPDATE'
            EXPORTING
              dyname     = sy-repid
              dynumb     = sy-dynnr
            TABLES
              dynpfields = gt_dynpfields.

        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

                      d~anrlt AS anrlt_t522t
              INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_sig_name)
              FROM pa0002 AS a LEFT JOIN pa9001 AS b ON a~pernr = b~pernr
                                                    AND b~begda <= @sy-datum
                                                    AND b~endda >= @sy-datum
                                                    AND ( b~subty = '0001'
                                                       OR b~subty = '0002'
                                                       OR b~subty = '0003' )
                               LEFT JOIN pa0001 AS c ON a~pernr = c~pernr
                                                    AND c~begda <= @sy-datum
                                                    AND c~endda >= @sy-datum
                               LEFT JOIN t522t  AS d ON a~anred = d~anred
                                                    AND d~sprsl = '2'
              WHERE a~pernr = @p_nam21
                AND a~begda <= @sy-datum
                AND a~endda >= @sy-datum.

        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          SORT lt_sig_name BY subty.
          READ TABLE lt_sig_name INTO DATA(lw_sig_name) INDEX 1.
          REFRESH: lt_sig_name.
          IF lw_sig_name-plans_9001 IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE tline
              INTO @DATA(lv_hrt1002_tline)
              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name-plans_9001 )
                AND subty  = '9001'
                AND endda >= @sy-datum
                AND begda <= @sy-datum.
          ELSEIF lw_sig_name-plans_0001 IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE tline
              INTO @lv_hrt1002_tline
              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name-plans_0001 )
                AND subty  = '9001'
                AND endda >= @sy-datum
                AND begda <= @sy-datum..
          ENDIF.

          IF lv_hrt1002_tline IS NOT INITIAL.
            t10_txt2 = lv_hrt1002_tline.
          ENDIF.

          IF lw_sig_name-knznm = '01'.
            CLEAR: gv_fullname.
            CALL FUNCTION 'ZHR_GET_TITLENAME'
              EXPORTING
                pernr     = p_nam21
              IMPORTING
                full_name = gv_fullname.
            MOVE gv_fullname TO t10_txt1.
          ELSE.
            IF lw_sig_name-vorsw_pa IS NOT INITIAL OR lw_sig_name-titl2_pa IS NOT INITIAL.
              t10_txt1 = lw_sig_name-vorsw_pa && lw_sig_name-titl2_pa.
            ELSE.
              t10_txt3 = lw_sig_name-anrlt_t522t.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

          t10_txt3 = t10_txt3 && lw_pa0002-vorna.
          t10_txt4 = lw_pa0002-nachn.

          CLEAR: gt_dynpfields[],
                 gs_dynpfields.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldname  = 'T10_TXT3'.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldvalue = t10_txt3.
          APPEND gs_dynpfields TO gt_dynpfields.

          CLEAR gs_dynpfields.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldname  = 'T10_TXT4'.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldvalue = t10_txt4.
          APPEND gs_dynpfields TO gt_dynpfields.

          CLEAR: gs_dynpfields.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldname  = 'T10_TXT1'.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldvalue = t10_txt1.
          APPEND gs_dynpfields TO gt_dynpfields.

          CLEAR gs_dynpfields.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldname  = 'T10_TXT2'.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldvalue = t10_txt2.
          APPEND gs_dynpfields TO gt_dynpfields.


          CALL FUNCTION 'DYNP_VALUES_UPDATE'
            EXPORTING
              dyname     = sy-repid
              dynumb     = sy-dynnr
            TABLES
              dynpfields = gt_dynpfields.

        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF screen-group1 = 'S22' AND p_nam21 IS NOT INITIAL AND gv_error_nam2 = 0.
      screen-input = 0.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

  CHECK p_prform IS INITIAL.

*<<< BOI CHG-25
  IF eban-bsart = '1A31' AND eban-zzmm_no_esign IS INITIAL.
    LOOP AT SCREEN.
      IF screen-group1 = 'SC1' OR screen-group1 = 'S21' OR screen-group1 = 'S22' OR screen-group1 = 'S23' OR screen-group1 = 'S24'.
        screen-input = 0.

  ELSEIF eban-bsart = '1INT'.

      IF screen-group1 = 'SC3'.
        screen-input = 0.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ELSE.
    LOOP AT SCREEN.
      IF screen-group1 = 'SC1'.
        screen-input = 1.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
      IF screen-group1 = 'S21' AND p_nam11 IS NOT INITIAL AND gv_error_nam1 = 0.
        screen-input = 0.
        MODIFY SCREEN.
      ENDIF.
      IF screen-group1 = 'S22' AND p_nam21 IS NOT INITIAL AND gv_error_nam2 = 0.
        screen-input = 0.
        MODIFY SCREEN.

              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name_tmp-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name_tmp-plans_9001 )
                AND subty  = '9001'
                AND endda >= @sy-datum
                AND begda <= @sy-datum.
          ELSEIF lw_sig_name_tmp-plans_0001 IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE tline
              INTO @lv_hrt1002_tline
              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name_tmp-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name_tmp-plans_0001 )
                AND subty  = '9001'
                AND endda >= @sy-datum
                AND begda <= @sy-datum.
          ENDIF.

          IF lv_hrt1002_tline IS NOT INITIAL.
            t13_txt2 = lv_hrt1002_tline.
          ENDIF.

            CALL FUNCTION 'ZHR_GET_TITLENAME'
              EXPORTING
                pernr     = p_nam11
              IMPORTING
                full_name = gv_fullname.
            MOVE gv_fullname TO t13_txt1.
          ELSE.
            IF lw_sig_name_tmp-vorsw_pa IS NOT INITIAL OR lw_sig_name_tmp-titl2_pa IS NOT INITIAL.
              t13_txt1 = lw_sig_name_tmp-vorsw_pa && lw_sig_name_tmp-titl2_pa.
            ELSE.
              t13_txt3 = lw_sig_name_tmp-anrlt_t522t.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

          t13_txt3 = t13_txt3 && lw_pa0002-vorna.
          t13_txt4 = lw_pa0002-nachn.

          CLEAR: gt_dynpfields[],
                 gs_dynpfields.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldname  = 'T13_TXT3'.
          gs_dynpfields-fieldvalue = t13_txt3.
          APPEND gs_dynpfields TO gt_dynpfields.

            TABLES

            PERFORM attach_form.  "INS CHG-26
          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.
        "<<<< CHG-24 : start insert
      ELSE.
        PERFORM pf_get_text.
        PERFORM pf_get_accass.
        PERFORM pf_chk_diff_fund.
        PERFORM pf_set_type_report.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_GET_HEADER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_get_header .
  DATA: lwa_weight    LIKE LINE OF gt_weight,    "CHG-17 INS
        lv_weight(10) TYPE c.                 "CHG-17 INS
*--->Get PR header

  SELECT banfn bsart "ekgrp
    FROM eban
    INTO TABLE gt_header
   WHERE banfn IN s_banfn
   GROUP BY banfn bsart ."ekgrp .
  SORT gt_weight BY banfn bnfpo zweight DESCENDING zseq ASCENDING. "CHG-17 INS
  LOOP AT gt_header.
    CLEAR eban.
    SELECT SINGLE * FROM eban
    WHERE banfn EQ gt_header-banfn.
    gt_header-ekgrp = eban-ekgrp.
********************************************************
* CHG-17 - START INS - Add status H: Header, I: Item

    CLEAR: gt_header-zweight_header, lv_weight.

    READ TABLE gt_weight INTO lwa_weight
    WITH KEY banfn = gt_header-banfn.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      IF lwa_weight-bnfpo IS INITIAL.
        gt_header-status = 'H'.
*        LOOP AT gt_weight INTO lwa_weight
*        WHERE banfn EQ gt_header-banfn.
*
*          WRITE lwa_weight-zweight TO lv_weight NO-ZERO.
*          CONDENSE lv_weight.
*          CONCATENATE lv_weight '%' INTO lv_weight.
**          lv_len = strlen( gt_header-zweight_header ).
*          IF gt_header-zweight_header IS INITIAL.
*            CONCATENATE 'เกณฑ์การพิจารณาคัดเลือกข้อเสนอ:'
*                        lwa_weight-ztext lv_weight
*            INTO gt_header-zweight_header
*            SEPARATED BY space.
*          ELSE.
*            CONCATENATE gt_header-zweight_header ','
*            INTO gt_header-zweight_header.
*            CONCATENATE gt_header-zweight_header
*                        lwa_weight-ztext lv_weight
*            INTO gt_header-zweight_header
*            SEPARATED BY space.
*
*          ENDIF.
*        ENDLOOP.
        READ TABLE gt_weight INTO lwa_weight WITH KEY zseq = '001'
                                                      zweight = 100.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          MOVE 'การพิจารณาคัดเลือกข้อเสนอโดยใช้เกณฑ์ราคา' TO gt_header-zweight_header.
        ELSE.
          MOVE 'การพิจารณาคัดเลือกข้อเสนอโดยใช้เกณฑ์ราคาประกอบเกณฑ์อื่น' TO gt_header-zweight_header.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        gt_header-status = 'I'.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      gt_header-status = ''.
    ENDIF.

* CHG-17 - END INS
********************************************************
    MODIFY gt_header.
  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_header BY banfn.
ENDFORM.                    " PF_GET_HEADER

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  pf_get_detail

*  <--  p2        text

FORM pf_get_detail.
  DATA: lwa_ekpo TYPE ekpo.
  DATA: lt_t024        TYPE TABLE OF t024 WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_fmfint      TYPE TABLE OF fmfint WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_tfkbt       TYPE TABLE OF tfkbt WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_fmfctrt     TYPE TABLE OF fmfctrt WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_detail1     LIKE gt_detail OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE,     "<<<CHG-05
        lt_detail2     LIKE gt_detail OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE,     "<<<CHG-05
        lt_detail_read LIKE gt_detail OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE. "<<<CHG-05
  DATA: lv_name1      TYPE lfa1-name1,                           "<<<CH-03
        lv_name2      TYPE lfa1-name2,
        lv_name3      TYPE lfa1-name3,
        lv_name4      TYPE lfa1-name4,
        lv_lifnr      TYPE lfa1-lifnr,
        lv_weight(10),                    "CHG-17 INS
        lwa_weight    LIKE LINE OF gt_weight. "CHG-17 INS
*--->Get PR item
  CHECK NOT gt_header[] IS INITIAL.
  SELECT
      eban~banfn
      eban~bnfpo
      eban~matnr
      eban~txz01
      eban~menge
      eban~meins
      eban~preis
      eban~peinh    "<<< CHG-01:INSERT
      eine~ebeln    "<<< CHG-02:INSERT
      eine~ebelp    "<<< CHG-02:INSERT
      eine~netpr
      eban~geber
      eban~fkber
      eban~fistl
      eban~kblnr
      eban~kblpos
      eban~afnam
      eban~badat
      eban~knttp
      eban~werks    " CHG-18 ADD
  INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_detail
      FROM eban
      LEFT JOIN eine
      ON eban~infnr = eine~infnr AND
         eban~ekorg = eine~ekorg AND
         eban~werks = eine~werks
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_header
    WHERE eban~banfn = gt_header-banfn
      AND eban~loekz <> 'X'.

  SORT gt_detail.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_detail.

*--->Get description
  SELECT *
    FROM fmfint
    INTO TABLE lt_fmfint
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
    WHERE spras = sy-langu
     AND  fincode = gt_detail-geber.

  SELECT *
    FROM tfkbt
    INTO TABLE lt_tfkbt
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
    WHERE spras = sy-langu
     AND  fkber = gt_detail-fkber.

  SELECT *
    FROM fmfctrt
    INTO TABLE lt_fmfctrt
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
    WHERE spras = sy-langu
     AND  fictr = gt_detail-fistl.
  SORT gt_weight BY banfn bnfpo zweight DESCENDING zseq ASCENDING. "CHG-17 INS

  LOOP AT gt_detail.
    SELECT SINGLE *
      INTO lwa_ekpo
      FROM ekpo
     WHERE ebeln = gt_detail-ebeln
       AND ebelp = gt_detail-ebelp.

    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      gt_detail-netpr = ( lwa_ekpo-effwr / lwa_ekpo-peinh * lwa_ekpo-bpumz / lwa_ekpo-bpumn ) / ( lwa_ekpo-menge * lwa_ekpo-umrez / lwa_ekpo-umren ).

    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE lt_fmfint WITH KEY fincode = gt_detail-geber.
    gt_detail-ge_des = lt_fmfint-beschr.

    READ TABLE lt_tfkbt WITH KEY fkber = gt_detail-fkber.
    gt_detail-fk_des = lt_tfkbt-fkbtx.

    READ TABLE lt_fmfctrt WITH KEY fictr = gt_detail-fistl.
    gt_detail-fi_des = lt_fmfctrt-beschr.
**************************************************************

* CHG-17 START INS - WEIGHT ITEM
    CLEAR gt_detail-zweight_item.

    LOOP AT gt_weight INTO lwa_weight
    WHERE banfn EQ gt_detail-banfn
    AND   bnfpo EQ gt_detail-bnfpo.
      CLEAR lv_weight.
      WRITE lwa_weight-zweight TO lv_weight NO-ZERO.
      CONDENSE lv_weight.
*      CONCATENATE '(' lv_weight '%)' INTO lv_weight.
      CONCATENATE lv_weight '%' INTO lv_weight.
      IF gt_detail-zweight_item IS INITIAL.
        CONCATENATE 'เกณฑ์การพิจารณาคัดเลือกข้อเสนอ:'
                    lwa_weight-ztext lv_weight
        INTO gt_detail-zweight_item
        SEPARATED BY space.
      ELSE.
        CONCATENATE gt_detail-zweight_item ','
        INTO gt_detail-zweight_item.
        CONCATENATE gt_detail-zweight_item
                    lwa_weight-ztext lv_weight
        INTO gt_detail-zweight_item
        SEPARATED BY space.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
* CHG-17 END INS
**************************************************************

    MODIFY gt_detail.
  ENDLOOP.


*--->Recheck data header and set date PR, Tel, Fax

  SELECT *
     INTO TABLE lt_t024
    FROM t024
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_header
    WHERE ekgrp = gt_header-ekgrp.

  "<<<CHG-05: Start Insert
  lt_detail1[] = gt_detail[].
  lt_detail2[] = gt_detail[].
  DELETE lt_detail1[] WHERE knttp <> ''.
  DELETE lt_detail2[] WHERE knttp = ''.
  IF lt_detail2[] IS INITIAL.
    lt_detail_read[] = lt_detail1[].
  ELSE.
    lt_detail_read[] = lt_detail2[].
  ENDIF.
  "<<<CHG-05: End Insert
  LOOP AT gt_header.
    CLEAR lv_lifnr.

    READ TABLE lt_detail_read WITH KEY banfn = gt_header-banfn.
*    READ TABLE gt_detail WITH KEY banfn = gt_header-banfn.  ">>>CHG-05: Delete
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      DELETE gt_header.
    ELSE.
      gt_header-knttp = lt_detail_read-knttp.                    ">>>CHG-05: Insert
      gt_header-badat = lt_detail_read-badat.                    ">>>CHG-05: Insert
*      gt_header-knttp = gt_detail-knttp.                    ">>>CHG-05: Delete
*      gt_header-badat = gt_detail-badat.                    ">>>CHG-05: Delete

      READ TABLE lt_t024 WITH KEY ekgrp = gt_header-ekgrp.

      CLEAR: ebkn,fmfctrt,j_1bbranch,adrc.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM eban
      WHERE  banfn = lt_detail_read-banfn
        AND  fistl NE space.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM fmfctrt
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
        AND fictr = eban-fistl.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM j_1bbranch
      WHERE branch =   gt_header-bsart+1(2).
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM adrc
      WHERE  addrnumber = j_1bbranch-adrnr.

      CONCATENATE fmfctrt-beschr adrc-name1
                   adrc-name2
      INTO gt_header-addr SEPARATED BY space.
*      gt_header-addr = lt_t0241-smtp_addr.

      IF lt_t024-tel_extens IS INITIAL.
        gt_header-tel = lt_t024-tel_number.
      ELSE.
        CONCATENATE lt_t024-tel_number '#' lt_t024-tel_extens
        INTO gt_header-tel.
      ENDIF.
      gt_header-fax = lt_t024-telfx.

*       SELECT banfn bsart ekgrp lifnr
*              FROM eban
*              INTO TABLE gt_header_F3
*              WHERE banfn IN s_banfn
*              and   banfn = gt_header-banfn.
      SELECT SINGLE lifnr
              FROM eban
              INTO lv_lifnr
              WHERE banfn IN s_banfn
              AND   banfn = gt_header-banfn.

      SELECT SINGLE  name1 name2 name3 name4                "<<<CH-03
             INTO    (lv_name1,lv_name2,lv_name3,lv_name4)
             FROM    lfa1
             WHERE    lifnr = lv_lifnr.

      CONCATENATE lv_name1 lv_name2 lv_name3 lv_name4 INTO gt_header-name. "SEPARATED BY space.
      gt_header-name1 = lv_name1.
      gt_header-name2 = lv_name2.
      gt_header-name3 = lv_name3.
      gt_header-name4 = lv_name4.

**********      MOVE m01 TO gt_header-sum2.       "<<<CH-03 วงเงิน
      MODIFY gt_header.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " pf_get_detail

            INTO ls_detail-b22_txz04 SEPARATED BY space.
          ELSE.
            ls_detail-b22_txz04 = wa_line-tdline.
          ENDIF.
        WHEN 5.
          IF ls_detail-b22_txz05 IS NOT INITIAL.
            CONCATENATE ls_detail-b22_txz05 wa_line-tdline
            INTO ls_detail-b22_txz05 SEPARATED BY space.
          ELSE.
            ls_detail-b22_txz05 = wa_line-tdline.
          ENDIF.
        WHEN 6.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_READ_TEXT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_read_text TABLES it_line STRUCTURE tline
                  USING VALUE(us_obj) TYPE c "Obj name
                        VALUE(us_typ) TYPE c "Type obj
                        VALUE(us_tab) TYPE c "Table name
                        VALUE(us_oln) TYPE c.               "X = 1 line
  DATA: lt_line TYPE TABLE OF tline WITH HEADER LINE."CHG-19
  DATA: lv_line   TYPE i, "CHG-19
        lv_strlen TYPE i, "CHG-19
        lv_str    TYPE string. "CHG-19
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <lfs_line> LIKE LINE OF  lt_line. "CHG-19
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <lfs_line2> LIKE LINE OF  lt_line. "CHG-19
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <lfs_line3> LIKE LINE OF  lt_line. "CHG-19
  FREE it_line.
*--->Check exist item text
  SELECT SINGLE * FROM stxh
   WHERE tdobject = us_tab
     AND tdname   = us_obj
     AND tdid     = us_typ
     AND tdspras  = sy-langu.

  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    FREE lt_line.
    CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
      EXPORTING
        id       = us_typ
        language = sy-langu
        name     = us_obj
        object   = us_tab
      TABLES
        lines    = lt_line.
    IF NOT lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
      IF us_oln = 'X'.
        APPEND LINES OF lt_line FROM 1 TO 1 TO it_line.
      ELSE.
*start comment   and add by "CHG-19
*        it_line[] = lt_line[].
        LOOP AT  lt_line ASSIGNING <lfs_line>.
          IF <lfs_line>-tdformat = '*'.
            APPEND INITIAL LINE TO it_line ASSIGNING <lfs_line2>.
            MOVE '*' TO <lfs_line2>-tdformat.
            MOVE <lfs_line>-tdline TO <lfs_line2>-tdline.
            lv_line = lv_line  + 1.
          ELSE.
            READ TABLE it_line ASSIGNING <lfs_line3> INDEX lv_line.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              CONCATENATE <lfs_line3>-tdline <lfs_line>-tdline INTO lv_str.
              lv_strlen =  strlen( lv_str ).
              IF lv_strlen > 132.
                <lfs_line3>-tdline = lv_str(132).
                lv_strlen =  lv_strlen - 132.
                APPEND INITIAL LINE TO it_line ASSIGNING <lfs_line2>.
                MOVE ' ' TO <lfs_line2>-tdformat.
                MOVE lv_str+132(lv_strlen) TO <lfs_line2>-tdline.
                lv_line = lv_line  + 1.
              ELSE.
                <lfs_line3>-tdline  = lv_str.
              ENDIF.

            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

        ENDLOOP.
*end comment   and add by "CHG-19
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " PF_READ_TEXT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_GET_ACCASS
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_get_accass .
  DATA: lt_fmfint  TYPE TABLE OF fmfint WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_tfkbt   TYPE TABLE OF tfkbt WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_fmfctrt TYPE TABLE OF fmfctrt WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_skat    TYPE TABLE OF skat WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_aufk    TYPE TABLE OF aufk WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_cskt    TYPE TABLE OF cskt WITH HEADER LINE.

  DATA: l_p1   TYPE setnode-setname,
        l_des1 TYPE setheadert-descript,
        l_p2   TYPE setnode-setname,
        l_des2 TYPE setheadert-descript.
*--->Get account assignment
  SELECT banfn
         bnfpo
         zebkn
         sakto
         kostl
         geber
         fkber
         fistl
         kblnr
         kblpos
         anln1
         anln2
         menge
         aufnr
  FROM ebkn
    INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE gt_accass
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
  WHERE banfn = gt_detail-banfn
    AND bnfpo = gt_detail-bnfpo.

  IF NOT gt_accass[] IS INITIAL.
*--->Get description
    SELECT *
      FROM fmfint
      INTO TABLE lt_fmfint
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
       AND  fincode = gt_accass-geber.

    SELECT *
      FROM tfkbt
      INTO TABLE lt_tfkbt
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
       AND  fkber = gt_accass-fkber.

    SELECT *
      FROM fmfctrt
      INTO TABLE lt_fmfctrt
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
       AND  fictr = gt_accass-fistl.

    SELECT *
      FROM skat
      INTO TABLE lt_skat
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
        AND ktopl = '1000'
        AND saknr = gt_accass-sakto.

    SELECT *
      FROM cskt
      INTO TABLE lt_cskt
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
        AND kokrs = '1000'
        AND kostl = gt_accass-kostl.

    SELECT *
      FROM aufk
      INTO TABLE lt_aufk
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE aufnr = gt_accass-aufnr.

    LOOP AT gt_accass.
      READ TABLE lt_fmfint WITH KEY fincode = gt_accass-geber.
      gt_accass-ge_des = lt_fmfint-beschr.

      READ TABLE lt_tfkbt WITH KEY fkber = gt_accass-fkber.
      gt_accass-fk_des = lt_tfkbt-fkbtx.

      READ TABLE lt_fmfctrt WITH KEY fictr = gt_accass-fistl.
      gt_accass-fi_des = lt_fmfctrt-beschr.

      READ TABLE lt_skat WITH KEY saknr = gt_accass-sakto.
      gt_accass-sa_des = lt_skat-txt50.

      READ TABLE lt_cskt WITH KEY kostl = gt_accass-kostl.
      gt_accass-ko_des = lt_cskt-ltext.

      READ TABLE lt_aufk WITH KEY aufnr = gt_accass-aufnr.
      gt_accass-ktext = lt_aufk-ktext.

*--->แผนหลัก และ แผนงาน
      CLEAR: l_p1, l_des1, l_p2, l_des2.
      CALL FUNCTION 'ZMM_FA_DESCRIPTION'
        EXPORTING
          key       = gt_accass-fkber
        IMPORTING
          p1        = l_p1
          des1      = l_des1
          p2        = l_p2
          des2      = l_des2
        EXCEPTIONS
          not_found = 1
          OTHERS    = 2.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gt_accass-plan1 = l_p1.
        gt_accass-p1_des = l_des1.
        gt_accass-plan2 = l_p2.
        gt_accass-p2_des = l_des2.
      ENDIF.

      MODIFY gt_accass.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

*--->Fill in description account assignment case data in EBAN
  READ TABLE gt_header WITH KEY knttp = ' '.
  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
*--->Get description
  SELECT *
    FROM fmfint
    INTO TABLE lt_fmfint
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
    WHERE spras = sy-langu
     AND  fincode = gt_detail-geber.

  SELECT *
    FROM tfkbt
    INTO TABLE lt_tfkbt
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
    WHERE spras = sy-langu
     AND  fkber = gt_detail-fkber.

  SELECT *
    FROM fmfctrt
    INTO TABLE lt_fmfctrt
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
    WHERE spras = sy-langu
     AND  fictr = gt_detail-fistl.

  LOOP AT gt_header
    WHERE knttp = ' '.
    LOOP AT gt_detail
      WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn
        AND seq = '1'.
      CLEAR gt_accass.
      MOVE: gt_detail-banfn TO gt_accass-banfn,
            gt_detail-bnfpo TO gt_accass-bnfpo,
            gt_detail-geber TO gt_accass-geber,
            gt_detail-fkber TO gt_accass-fkber,
            gt_detail-fistl TO gt_accass-fistl.

      READ TABLE lt_fmfint WITH KEY fincode = gt_accass-geber.
      gt_accass-ge_des = lt_fmfint-beschr.

      READ TABLE lt_tfkbt WITH KEY fkber = gt_accass-fkber.
      gt_accass-fk_des = lt_tfkbt-fkbtx.

      READ TABLE lt_fmfctrt WITH KEY fictr = gt_accass-fistl.
      gt_accass-fi_des = lt_fmfctrt-beschr.

*--->แผนหลัก และ แผนงาน
      CLEAR: l_p1, l_des1, l_p2, l_des2.
      CALL FUNCTION 'ZMM_FA_DESCRIPTION'
        EXPORTING
          key       = gt_accass-fkber
        IMPORTING
          p1        = l_p1
          des1      = l_des1
          p2        = l_p2
          des2      = l_des2
        EXCEPTIONS
          not_found = 1
          OTHERS    = 2.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gt_accass-plan1 = l_p1.
        gt_accass-p1_des = l_des1.
        gt_accass-plan2 = l_p2.
        gt_accass-p2_des = l_des2.
      ENDIF.

      APPEND gt_accass.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
*Sort data
  SORT gt_accass BY banfn bnfpo zebkn.
ENDFORM.                    " PF_GET_ACCASS

*                                       gt_header-banfn.
*      WHEN '4'.
*        gt_header-meth = 'พิเศษ 17(2)'.
*        PERFORM pf_fillin_result USING gt_header-bsart+3(1)
*                                       gt_header-banfn.
*      WHEN '5'.
*        gt_header-meth = 'กรณีพิเศษ 17(3)(4)'.
*        PERFORM pf_fillin_result USING gt_header-bsart+3(1)
*                                       gt_header-banfn.
**>>> CHG-04 : Start of insertion
*      WHEN '7'.
*        gt_header-meth = 'ตกลง 34(1), 47(1)'.
*        PERFORM pf_fillin_result USING gt_header-bsart+3(1)
*                                       gt_header-banfn.
*      WHEN '8'.
*        gt_header-meth = 'คัดเลือก 34(2)'.
*        PERFORM pf_fillin_result USING gt_header-bsart+3(1)
*                                       gt_header-banfn.
*      WHEN '9'.
*        gt_header-meth = 'คัดเลือกจำกัดฯ 47(2)'.
*        PERFORM pf_fillin_result USING gt_header-bsart+3(1)
*                                       gt_header-banfn.
**<<< CHG-04 : End of insertion
*    ENDCASE.
    MODIFY gt_header.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " PF_SET_TYPE_REPORT

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_FILLIN_DETAIL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_fillin_detail .
  DATA: lt_line TYPE TABLE OF tline WITH HEADER LINE.

  DATA: ls_detail1 LIKE gt_detail,
        l_seq      TYPE i,
        l_cnt      TYPE i.
  DATA: l_obj TYPE thead-tdname.

*--->Prepart itab for show detail
  LOOP AT gt_detail INTO ls_detail1.       "CHG-18 DEL
    "  LOOP AT gt_detail_tmp INTO ls_detail1.    "CHG-18 ADD
    IF NOT ( ls_detail1-peinh EQ '9'        "CHG-15 Change
       AND ls_detail1-badat IS INITIAL      "CHG-15 add condition del menge
       AND ls_detail1-menge EQ 0 ).         "CHG-15 add condition del menge
      APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
*  gt_detail1[] = gt_detail[].

  SORT gt_detail BY banfn bnfpo ASCENDING seq DESCENDING.      "CHG-18 DEL
*  SORT gt_detail_tmp BY banfn bnfpo ASCENDING seq DESCENDING.  "CHG-18 ADD
  LOOP AT gt_detail1
    WHERE seq = 1.

    CLEAR: ls_detail1, l_seq, l_cnt.
*Set PR, Item
    MOVE: gt_detail1-banfn TO ls_detail1-banfn,
          gt_detail1-bnfpo TO ls_detail1-bnfpo.

*Check last seq per docment
    READ TABLE gt_detail WITH KEY banfn = gt_detail1-banfn     "CHG-18 DEL
                                  bnfpo = gt_detail1-bnfpo.    "CHG-18 DEL
*    READ TABLE gt_detail_tmp WITH KEY banfn = gt_detail1-banfn  "CHG-18 ADD
*                                      bnfpo = gt_detail1-bnfpo. "CHG-18 ADD
    l_seq = gt_detail-seq.        "CHG-18 DEL
*    l_seq = gt_detail_tmp-seq.    "CHG-18 ADD

*Material
    IF NOT gt_detail1-matnr IS INITIAL.

*      CONCATENATE 'รหัสสินค้า:' gt_detail1-matnr
*              INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*              SEPARATED BY space.

      ls_detail1-txz01 = 'รหัสสินค้า:'.
*      ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_detail1-matnr.
      CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_OUTPUT'
        EXPORTING
          input  = gt_detail1-matnr
        IMPORTING
          output = ls_detail1-txz02.


*      ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-sa_des.
      ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
      l_seq = l_seq + 1.
      ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
      APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
    ENDIF.

*--->Check data fund in eban
    IF gt_detail1-geber IS INITIAL.
*==============Case data in EBKN==============
      LOOP AT gt_accass
        WHERE banfn = gt_detail1-banfn
          AND bnfpo = gt_detail1-bnfpo.

        l_cnt = l_cnt + 1.
*--->Check multi item
        IF l_cnt > 2.

          MOVE '**รายการบัญชีมากกว่า 2 รายการ ตรวจสอบได้จากระบบ**' TO
          ls_detail1-txz01.
          l_seq = l_seq + 1.
          ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
          APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
          EXIT.
        ELSE.
*G/L
          IF NOT gt_accass-sakto IS INITIAL.
            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'บัญชี:'.
            ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_accass-sakto.
            ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-sa_des.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*            CONCATENATE 'บัญชี:' gt_accass-sa_des
*                INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*                SEPARATED BY space.
**            MOVE gt_accass-menge TO ls_detail1-menge.
            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR ls_detail1-menge.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
                  ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
          ENDIF.
*ศูนย์ต้นทุน
          IF NOT gt_accass-kostl IS INITIAL.
            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'ศูนย์ต้นทุน:'.
            ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_accass-kostl.
            ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-ko_des.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*            CONCATENATE 'ศูนย์ต้นทุน:' gt_accass-ko_des
*                INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*                SEPARATED BY space.
            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
                  ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
          ENDIF.
*รหัสสินทรัพย์
          IF NOT gt_accass-anln1 IS INITIAL.
            CLEAR anla.
            SELECT SINGLE * FROM anla
            WHERE anln1 = gt_accass-anln1
              AND anln2 = gt_accass-anln2.                      "CHG-14

            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'รหัสสินทรัพย์:'.
            ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_accass-anln1.
            ls_detail1-txz03 = anla-txt50.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*            CONCATENATE 'รหัสสินทรัพย์:' gt_accass-anln1 gt_accass-anln2
*               INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*               SEPARATED BY space.
            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
                  ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
          ENDIF.
*กองทุน
          IF NOT gt_accass-geber IS INITIAL.
            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'กองทุน:'.
            ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_accass-geber.
            ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-ge_des.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*            CONCATENATE 'กองทุน:' gt_accass-ge_des
*                INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*                SEPARATED BY space.
            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,

          ENDIF.
*แผนงาน/โครงการ
          IF NOT gt_accass-fkber IS INITIAL.
            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'แผนงาน/โครงการ:'.
            ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_accass-fkber.
            ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-fk_des.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*            CONCATENATE 'แผนงาน/โครงการ:' gt_accass-fk_des
*                INTO ls_detail1-txz01

            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
                  ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
          ENDIF.
*ศูนย์เงินทุน
          IF NOT gt_accass-fistl IS INITIAL.
            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'ศูนย์เงินทุน:'.
            ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_accass-fistl.
            ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-fi_des.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*            CONCATENATE 'ศูนย์เงินทุน:' gt_accass-fi_des
*               INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*               SEPARATED BY space.
            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
                  ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
          ENDIF.
* Start of insert: CHG-12
* ใบสั่งงาน
          IF NOT gt_accass-aufnr IS INITIAL.
            l_obj = gt_accass-aufnr.
            PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                                  USING l_obj 'LTXT' 'AUFK' ' '.
            READ TABLE lt_line INDEX 1.
            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'ใบสั่งงาน:'.
            WRITE gt_accass-aufnr TO ls_detail1-txz02 NO-ZERO.
            IF lt_line-tdline IS NOT INITIAL.
              ls_detail1-txz03 = lt_line-tdline.
            ELSE.
              ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-ktext.
            ENDIF.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
                  ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
          ENDIF.
* End of insert: CHG-12
*Earmarked fund
          IF NOT gt_accass-kblnr IS INITIAL.
            CLEAR kblp.
            SELECT SINGLE * FROM kblp
            WHERE belnr = gt_accass-kblnr
              AND blpos = gt_accass-kblpos.

            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'Earmarked fund:'.
            ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_accass-kblnr.
            ls_detail1-txz03 = kblp-ptext.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.

*            CONCATENATE 'Earmarked fund:' gt_accass-kblnr
*            gt_accass-kblpos
*               INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*               SEPARATED BY space.
            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
                  ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
          ENDIF.

        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

    ELSE.
*==============Case data in EBAN==============
*กองทุน
      IF NOT gt_detail1-geber IS INITIAL.
        ls_detail1-txz01 = 'กองทุน:'.
        ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_detail1-geber.
        ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_detail1-ge_des.
        ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*        CONCATENATE 'กองทุน:' gt_detail1-ge_des
*            INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*            SEPARATED BY space.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
        APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
        CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
              ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
      ENDIF.
*แผนงาน/โครงการ
      IF NOT gt_detail1-fkber IS INITIAL.
        ls_detail1-txz01 = 'แผนงาน/โครงการ:'.
        ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_detail1-fkber.
        ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_detail1-fk_des.
        ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*        CONCATENATE 'แผนงาน/โครงการ:' gt_detail1-fk_des
*            INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*            SEPARATED BY space.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
        APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
        CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
              ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
      ENDIF.
*ศูนย์เงินทุน
      IF NOT gt_detail1-fistl IS INITIAL.
        ls_detail1-txz01 = 'ศูนย์เงินทุน:'.
        ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_detail1-fistl.
        ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_detail1-fi_des.
        ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*        CONCATENATE 'ศูนย์เงินทุน:' gt_detail1-fi_des
*           INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*           SEPARATED BY space.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
        APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
        CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
              ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
      ENDIF.
*Earmarked fund
      IF NOT gt_detail1-kblnr IS INITIAL.
        CLEAR kblp.
        SELECT SINGLE * FROM kblp
        WHERE belnr = gt_accass-kblnr
          AND blpos = gt_accass-kblpos.

        ls_detail1-txz01 = 'Earmarked fund:'.
        ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_detail1-kblnr.
        ls_detail1-txz03 = kblp-ptext.
        ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*        CONCATENATE 'Earmarked fund:' gt_detail1-kblnr gt_detail1-kblpos
*           INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*           SEPARATED BY space.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
        APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
        CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
              ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.

*ผู้ขอ
    IF NOT gt_detail1-afnam IS INITIAL.
      ls_detail1-txz01 = 'ผู้ขอ:'.
      ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_detail1-afnam.
*       ls_detail1-txz03 = KBLP-ptext.
      ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*      CONCATENATE 'ผู้ขอ:' gt_detail1-afnam
*         INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*         SEPARATED BY space.
      l_seq = l_seq + 1.
      ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
      APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
    ENDIF.
*Comment
    CONCATENATE gt_detail1-banfn gt_detail1-bnfpo
           INTO l_obj.
*    CLEAR L_LINE.
*<-- CH11
*    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
*                         USING l_obj 'B02' 'EBAN' ' '.
*    LOOP AT lt_line
*        WHERE tdline <> ' '.
*      l_seq = l_seq + 1.
*      ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
*      IF sy-tabix = 1.
*        CONCATENATE 'หมายเหตุรายการ:' lt_line-tdline
*               INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*             SEPARATED BY space.
*      ELSE.
*        MOVE lt_line-tdline TO ls_detail1-txz01.
*      ENDIF.
*      APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
*    ENDLOOP.

    CLEAR: lt_line,v_fieldfirst.
    v_fieldcount = 1.
    CLEAR ls_detail1.

    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                             USING l_obj 'B02' 'EBAN' ' '.
    LOOP AT lt_line INTO wa_line.
*        WHERE tdline <> ' '.                                     "CHG-12

      IF wa_line-tdformat = '*'.
*        IF ls_detail1-txz01 NE space.                            "CHG-12
        IF ls_detail1-banfn NE space.                             "CHG-12
          APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR ls_detail1.
        MOVE: gt_detail1-banfn TO ls_detail1-banfn,
        gt_detail1-bnfpo TO ls_detail1-bnfpo.
        v_fieldcount = 1.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
      ENDIF.

      ls_detail1-peinh = '9'.
      CASE v_fieldcount.
        WHEN 1.
          IF v_fieldfirst = 0.
            CONCATENATE 'หมายเหตุรายการ:' wa_line-tdline "CH11
            INTO ls_detail1-txz01
            SEPARATED BY space.
            v_fieldfirst = 1.
          ELSE.
            ls_detail1-txz01 =  wa_line-tdline.
          ENDIF.
        WHEN 2.
          MOVE wa_line-tdline TO ls_detail1-txz02.
        WHEN 3.
          MOVE wa_line-tdline TO ls_detail1-txz03.
        WHEN 4.
          MOVE wa_line-tdline TO ls_detail1-txz04.
        WHEN 5.
          MOVE wa_line-tdline TO ls_detail1-txz05.
        WHEN 6.
          MOVE wa_line-tdline TO ls_detail1-txz06.
        WHEN 7.
          MOVE wa_line-tdline TO ls_detail1-txz07.
        WHEN 8.
          MOVE wa_line-tdline TO ls_detail1-txz08.

      ENDCASE.

      v_fieldcount = v_fieldcount + 1.
      AT LAST.
        APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
        CLEAR ls_detail1.
        MOVE: gt_detail1-banfn TO ls_detail1-banfn,
      gt_detail1-bnfpo TO ls_detail1-bnfpo.
        v_fieldcount = 1.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
      ENDAT.


    ENDLOOP.

* CH11 -->


  ENDLOOP.

*---Sort itab
  SORT gt_detail1 BY banfn bnfpo seq.
  SORT gt_detail BY banfn bnfpo seq.

************************************CHG-21: Insert Begin
  CLEAR gv_reccnt.
  LOOP AT it_reccount ASSIGNING <wa_reccount>.
    <wa_reccount>-reccnt = <wa_reccount>-reccnt + gv_reccnt.
    LOOP AT gt_detail1 WHERE banfn = <wa_reccount>-banfn AND bnfpo = <wa_reccount>-bnfpo.
      gv_reccnt = gv_reccnt + 1.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
  SORT it_reccount BY reccnt DESCENDING.

  LOOP AT it_reccount INTO wa_reccount.
    LOOP AT gt_detail1 WHERE banfn = wa_reccount-banfn AND bnfpo = wa_reccount-bnfpo.
      IF sy-tabix > wa_reccount-reccnt AND gt_detail1-txz01 IS INITIAL AND gt_detail1-txz02 IS INITIAL
                                       AND gt_detail1-txz03 IS INITIAL AND gt_detail1-txz04 IS INITIAL
                                       AND gt_detail1-txz05 IS INITIAL AND gt_detail1-txz06 IS INITIAL
                                       AND gt_detail1-txz07 IS INITIAL AND gt_detail1-txz08 IS INITIAL.
        DELETE gt_detail1 INDEX sy-tabix.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
************************************CHG-21: Insert End

  DELETE gt_detail1 WHERE txz01 IS INITIAL
                      AND txz02 IS INITIAL
                      AND txz03 IS INITIAL
                      AND txz04 IS INITIAL
                      AND txz05 IS INITIAL
                      AND txz06 IS INITIAL
                      AND txz07 IS INITIAL
                      AND txz08 IS INITIAL
                      AND ( b22_txz01 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b22_txz02 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b22_txz03 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b22_txz04 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b22_txz05 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b22_txz06 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b22_txz07 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b22_txz08 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b23_txz01 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b23_txz02 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b23_txz03 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b23_txz04 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b23_txz05 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b23_txz06 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b23_txz07 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b23_txz08 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b25_txz01 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b25_txz02 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b25_txz03 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b25_txz04 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b25_txz05 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b25_txz06 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b25_txz07 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b25_txz08 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b24_txz01 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b24_txz02 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b24_txz03 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b24_txz04 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b24_txz05 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b24_txz06 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b24_txz07 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b24_txz08 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b26_txz01 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b26_txz02 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b26_txz03 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b26_txz04 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b26_txz05 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b26_txz06 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b26_txz07 IS NOT INITIAL
                         OR b26_txz08 IS NOT INITIAL ).


ENDFORM.                    " PF_FILLIN_DETAIL

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_FILLIN_COMMITTEE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_fillin_committee .
  DATA: lt_cmit  TYPE TABLE OF ztmmprcommittee WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_dd07t TYPE TABLE OF dd07t WITH HEADER LINE.
*--->Get data from committee table
  SELECT *
  FROM ztmmprcommittee
  INTO TABLE lt_cmit
FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_header
    WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn
      AND committee_group IN ('01', '02').
  CHECK NOT lt_cmit[] IS INITIAL.
*--->Get all position committee
  SELECT *
    FROM dd07t
   INTO TABLE lt_dd07t
   WHERE domname = 'ZPOSITION'
     AND ddlanguage = sy-langu.

*--->Fill in all text name
  LOOP AT lt_cmit.

    MOVE: lt_cmit-banfn TO gt_comt-banfn,
          lt_cmit-bnfpo TO gt_comt-bnfpo.
* Start of comment: CHG-13
*    IF NOT lt_cmit-vorna IS INITIAL.
*      IF NOT lt_cmit-plans IS INITIAL.
*        CONCATENATE lt_cmit-vorna lt_cmit-nach2
*               INTO gt_comt-txt1 SEPARATED BY space.
*        CONCATENATE lt_cmit-plans '(' gt_comt-txt1 ')' INTO gt_comt-txt1.
*      ELSE.
*        CONCATENATE lt_cmit-vorna lt_cmit-nach2 INTO gt_comt-txt1
*       SEPARATED BY space.
*      ENDIF.
*
*      READ TABLE lt_dd07t WITH KEY valpos+2(2) = lt_cmit-zposition.
*      MOVE lt_dd07t-ddtext TO gt_comt-txt2.
*    ELSE.
*      MOVE lt_cmit-plans TO gt_comt-txt1.
*      READ TABLE lt_dd07t WITH KEY valpos+2(2) = lt_cmit-zposition.
*      MOVE lt_dd07t-ddtext TO gt_comt-txt2.
*    ENDIF.
* End of comment: CHG-13

* Start of insert: CHG-13
    IF lt_cmit-plans IS NOT INITIAL.
      gt_comt-plans = lt_cmit-plans.
      IF lt_cmit-vorna IS NOT INITIAL OR
         lt_cmit-nach2 IS NOT INITIAL.
        CONCATENATE '(' lt_cmit-vorna lt_cmit-nach2 ')'
        INTO gt_comt-txt1 SEPARATED BY space.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      CONCATENATE lt_cmit-vorna lt_cmit-nach2
      INTO gt_comt-txt1 SEPARATED BY space.
    ENDIF.

    READ TABLE lt_dd07t WITH KEY valpos+2(2) = lt_cmit-zposition.
    MOVE lt_dd07t-ddtext TO gt_comt-txt2.
* End of insert: CHG-13

    MOVE: lt_cmit-zposition TO gt_comt-position. "<<<CHG_09: Insert
    APPEND gt_comt.
    CLEAR gt_comt.                                              "CHG-13
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.                    " PF_FILLIN_COMMITTEE

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_FILLIN_TEXT

*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text

FORM pf_fillin_text .
  DATA: lt_line TYPE TABLE OF tline WITH HEADER LINE.
  DATA: l_obj      TYPE thead-tdname,
        l_obj_tmp  TYPE thead-tdname,                  "INS CHG-25
        l_obj_tmp2 TYPE thead-tdname,                  "INS CHG-25
*        l_line     TYPE tline-tdline.                 "DEL CHG-25
        l_line     TYPE string.                        "INS CHG-25

*--->Get text first line item is active
  gt_tmp[] = gt_detail[].

  SORT gt_tmp BY banfn bnfpo.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_tmp
                   COMPARING banfn.
  LOOP AT gt_tmp.
    CLEAR gt_text.
    MOVE: gt_tmp-banfn TO gt_text-banfn,
          gt_tmp-bnfpo TO gt_text-bnfpo.

    CONCATENATE gt_tmp-banfn gt_tmp-bnfpo
           INTO l_obj.


*<<< Start of INS CHG-25

    l_obj_tmp = gt_tmp-banfn.

*<<< BOE CHG-25
*    CLEAR l_line.
*    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
*                             USING l_obj 'B13' 'EBAN' ' '.
*    IF lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
*      g_flag = 'N'.
**      EXIT.
*    ELSE.
*      LOOP AT lt_line.
*        MOVE: 'B13' TO gt_text-type.
*        IF lt_line-tdformat = '*' AND sy-tabix <> 1.
*          APPEND gt_text.
*          gt_text-txt3_2 = lt_line-tdline.
*        ELSE.
*          CONCATENATE gt_text-txt3_2 lt_line-tdline INTO gt_text-txt3_2.
*        ENDIF.
*      ENDLOOP.
*      CONDENSE gt_text-txt3_2.
*      IF gt_text-txt3_2 IS NOT INITIAL.
*        APPEND gt_text.
*      ENDIF.
*      CLEAR gt_text-txt3_2.
*    ENDIF.
    CLEAR l_line.
    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                             USING l_obj 'B13' 'EBAN' ' '.
    IF lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
      g_flag = 'N'.
    ELSE.
      DESCRIBE TABLE lt_line LINES DATA(lv_lines).
      IF lv_lines <> 1.
*        LOOP AT lt_line.
**        WHERE tdline <> ' '.
*          MOVE: 'B13' TO gt_text-type,
*                lt_line-tdline TO gt_text-txt3_2.
*          APPEND gt_text.
*          CLEAR gt_text-txt3_2.
*        ENDLOOP.
        LOOP AT lt_line.
          IF lt_line-tdformat = '*'.
            IF sy-tabix <> 1.
              APPEND gt_text.
              CLEAR gt_text-txt3_2.
            ENDIF.
            MOVE: lt_line-tdline TO gt_text-txt3_2.
          ELSE.
            CONCATENATE gt_text-txt3_2 lt_line-tdline INTO gt_text-txt3_2.
          ENDIF.
          MOVE: 'B13' TO gt_text-type.
        ENDLOOP.
        IF gt_text-txt3_2 IS NOT INITIAL.
          APPEND gt_text.
          CLEAR gt_text-txt3_2.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        LOOP AT lt_line.
          MOVE: 'B13' TO gt_text-type.
          CONCATENATE gt_text-txt3_2 lt_line-tdline INTO gt_text-txt3_2.
        ENDLOOP.
        APPEND gt_text.
        CLEAR gt_text-txt3_2.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR l_line.
    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                             USING l_obj 'B14' 'EBAN' ' '.
    IF lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
      g_flag = 'N'.
    ELSE.
      DESCRIBE TABLE lt_line LINES lv_lines.
      IF lv_lines <> 1.
*        LOOP AT lt_line.
**        WHERE tdline <> ' '.
*          MOVE: 'B14' TO gt_text-type,
*                lt_line-tdline TO gt_text-txt1.
*          APPEND gt_text.
*          CLEAR gt_text-txt1.
*        ENDLOOP.
        LOOP AT lt_line.
          IF lt_line-tdformat = '*'.
            IF sy-tabix <> 1.
              APPEND gt_text.
              CLEAR gt_text-txt1.
            ENDIF.
            MOVE: lt_line-tdline TO gt_text-txt1.
          ELSE.
            CONCATENATE gt_text-txt1 lt_line-tdline INTO gt_text-txt1.
          ENDIF.
          MOVE: 'B14' TO gt_text-type.
        ENDLOOP.
        IF gt_text-txt1 IS NOT INITIAL.
          APPEND gt_text.
          CLEAR gt_text-txt1.
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        LOOP AT lt_line.
          MOVE: 'B14' TO gt_text-type.
          CONCATENATE gt_text-txt1 lt_line-tdline INTO gt_text-txt1.
        ENDLOOP.
        APPEND gt_text.
        CLEAR gt_text-txt1.
      ENDIF.
*      LOOP AT lt_line WHERE tdline <> ' '.
*        MOVE: 'B14' TO gt_text-type.
*        IF lt_line-tdformat = '*'.
*          IF gt_text-txt1 IS NOT INITIAL.
*            APPEND gt_text.
*            CLEAR gt_text-txt1.
*          ENDIF.
*          MOVE lt_line-tdline TO l_line.
*        ELSE.

    ENDIF.
*>>> EOE CHG-25

    CLEAR l_line.

                             USING l_obj_tmp 'B02' 'EBANH' ' '.
    IF NOT lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
      LOOP AT lt_line.
        MOVE: 'B2H' TO gt_text-type.
        IF lt_line-tdformat = '*' AND sy-tabix <> 1.
          APPEND gt_text.
          gt_text-txt1 = lt_line-tdline.

        ENDIF.

      IF gt_text-txt1 IS NOT INITIAL.
        APPEND gt_text.
      ENDIF.
      CLEAR gt_text-txt1.
    ENDIF.

      FROM ztmmdepartment

    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      MOVE: 'DES' TO gt_text-type.
      APPEND gt_text.
      CLEAR: gt_text-txt1, gt_text-zdesc.
    ENDIF.

      FROM ztmmprweight
      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_ztmmprweight_tmp)
      WHERE banfn = @gt_tmp-banfn.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.

      READ TABLE lt_ztmmprweight_tmp INTO DATA(lw_ztmmprweight) INDEX 1.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.

              lw_ztmmprweight-ztext TO gt_text-ztext.

        CLEAR: gt_text-ztext, gt_text-zdesc.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

      FROM ztmmdoctypedesc
      INTO gt_text-ztype
      WHERE pr_method = gt_header-bsart.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      MOVE: 'TYP' TO gt_text-type.
      APPEND gt_text.
      CLEAR gt_text-ztype.
    ENDIF.

    LOOP AT gt_detail INTO DATA(lw_detail) WHERE banfn = gt_tmp-banfn.
      CONCATENATE lw_detail-banfn lw_detail-bnfpo
       INTO l_obj_tmp2.
      REFRESH lt_line.
      CLEAR l_line.
      PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                               USING l_obj_tmp2 'B10' 'EBAN' ' '.
      IF lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
        g_flag = 'N'.
*        EXIT.
      ELSE.
        LOOP AT lt_line
          WHERE tdline <> ' '.
          CONCATENATE l_line lt_line-tdline INTO l_line.
        ENDLOOP.
        CONDENSE l_line.
        MOVE: 'B10' TO gt_text-type,
              l_line TO gt_text-txt1,
              lw_detail-bnfpo TO gt_text-bnfpo,
              lw_detail-banfn TO gt_text-banfn.
        APPEND gt_text.
        CLEAR gt_text-txt1.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
*>>> End of INS CHG-25


    CLEAR l_line.
    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                          USING l_obj 'B05' 'EBAN' ' '.
*    IF LT_LINE[] IS INITIAL.
*      G_FLAG = 'N'.
*      EXIT.
*    ELSE.
    IF NOT lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
      LOOP AT lt_line
        WHERE tdline <> ' '.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  pf_get_ZTMMPR_APPROVE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_get_ztmmpr_approve.
  REFRESH it_ztmmpr_approve.
  SELECT *
    INTO TABLE it_ztmmpr_approve
    FROM ztmmpr_approve
    WHERE banfn IN s_banfn.
ENDFORM.                    "pf_get_ZTMMPR_APPROVE

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  pf_get_subject
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_get_subject .
*--->Set subject header
  CASE 'X'.
    WHEN r1.
      g_subj = 'ใบขอซื้อ'.
    WHEN r2.
      g_subj = 'ใบขอจ้าง'.
    WHEN r3.
      g_subj = 'ใบขอเช่า'.
  ENDCASE.

ENDFORM.                    " pf_get_subject

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  display_approve
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM display_approve.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <header> LIKE LINE OF gt_header.

  DATA: l_callback  TYPE sy-repid,
        lt_fieldcat TYPE slis_t_fieldcat_alv,
        lt_events   TYPE slis_t_event,
        lt_sort     TYPE slis_t_sortinfo_alv,
        wa_layout   TYPE slis_layout_alv.

  DATA: lt_approve      TYPE TABLE OF ztmmpr_approve.
  DATA: lwa_approve   TYPE ztmmpr_approve,
        lwa_approve_t TYPE ztmmpr_approve.
  DATA: v_name4 TYPE name4_gp.

  CHECK NOT c2 IS INITIAL.


  lt_approve[] = it_ztmmpr_approve[].
  REFRESH it_ztmmpr_approve.
  SORT lt_approve BY banfn.

  LOOP AT gt_header.
    CLEAR lwa_approve.

    lwa_approve-banfn = gt_header-banfn.
*<<< BOI CHG-25
    LOOP AT gt_text WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn
                      AND type = 'B99'.
      DATA(lv_len) = strlen( gt_text-txt2 ).
      IF lv_len > 128.
        lwa_approve-item_desc = gt_text-txt2(128).
        lwa_approve-item_desc_2 = gt_text-txt2+128.
      ELSE.
        lwa_approve-item_desc = gt_text-txt2.
      ENDIF.
*      IF lwa_approve-item_desc IS INITIAL.
*        lwa_approve-item_desc = gt_text-txt2.
*      ELSE.
*        lwa_approve-item_desc_2 = gt_text-txt2.
*      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
*    READ TABLE gt_text WITH KEY banfn = gt_header-banfn
*                                type = 'B07'.
*    IF sy-subrc = 0.
*      lwa_approve-item_desc = gt_text-txt2.
*    ENDIF.
*>>> EOI CHG-25

*    READ TABLE lt_approve INTO lwa_approve_t                                 "DEL CHG-25
*      WITH KEY banfn = gt_header-banfn.                                      "DEL CHG-25
    LOOP AT lt_approve INTO lwa_approve_t WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn.      "INS CHG-25
*    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      lwa_approve-item_desc = lwa_approve_t-item_desc.                        "INS CHG-25
      lwa_approve-item_desc_2 = lwa_approve_t-item_desc_2.                        "INS CHG-25
      lwa_approve-appv_no = lwa_approve_t-appv_no.                            "INS CHG-25
      lwa_approve-name1 = lwa_approve_t-name1.
      lwa_approve-name2 = lwa_approve_t-name2.
      lwa_approve-name3 = lwa_approve_t-name3.
      lwa_approve-name4 = lwa_approve_t-name4.
      lwa_approve-amt   = lwa_approve_t-amt.
      APPEND lwa_approve TO it_ztmmpr_approve.                                "INS CHG-25
    ENDLOOP.                                                                  "INS CHG-25
*    IF it_ztmmpr_approve[] IS INITIAL.                                        "INS CHG-25

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM PF_FIELD_CAT                                             *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ........                                                      *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  TB_FCAT                                                       *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_field_cat USING  tb_fcat TYPE slis_t_fieldcat_alv.

  DATA: wa_fieldcat LIKE LINE OF tb_fcat,
        l_col_pos   TYPE i.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS <fcat>  LIKE LINE OF tb_fcat.

  DEFINE field_cat.
    CLEAR wa_fieldcat.
    ADD 1 TO l_col_pos.
    wa_fieldcat-fieldname     = &1.
    wa_fieldcat-ref_tabname   = &2.
    wa_fieldcat-col_pos       = l_col_pos.
    wa_fieldcat-key           = &3.
    wa_fieldcat-no_out        = &4.
    wa_fieldcat-datatype      = &5.
    wa_fieldcat-ref_fieldname = &6.
    wa_fieldcat-ddictxt       = 'L'.
    IF NOT &7 IS INITIAL.
      wa_fieldcat-seltext_l     = &7.
      wa_fieldcat-seltext_m     = &7.
      wa_fieldcat-seltext_s     = &7.
    ENDIF.
    wa_fieldcat-edit            = &8.
    IF NOT &9 IS INITIAL.
      wa_fieldcat-outputlen    = &9.
    ENDIF.
    APPEND wa_fieldcat TO tb_fcat.
  END-OF-DEFINITION.

  field_cat 'BANFN' 'ZTMMPR_APPROVE' 'X' '' '' 'BANFN' '' '' ''.
  field_cat 'APPV_NO' 'ZTMMPR_APPROVE' '' '' '' 'APPV_NO' 'เลขที่รายงานผลอนุมัติ' 'X' '20'.
  field_cat 'ITEM_DESC' 'ZTMMPR_APPROVE' '' '' '' 'ITEM_DESC' 'รายการพิจารณา' 'X' '50'.   "INS CHG-25
  field_cat 'ITEM_DESC_2' 'ZTMMPR_APPROVE' '' '' '' 'ITEM_DESC' 'รายการพิจารณา(ต่อ)' 'X' '50'.   "INS CHG-25
  field_cat 'NAME1' 'ZTMMPR_APPROVE' ''  '' '' 'NAME1' 'ผู้ขาย' 'X' ''.
  field_cat 'NAME2' 'ZTMMPR_APPROVE' ''  '' '' 'NAME2' 'ผู้ขาย (ต่อ1)' 'X' ''.
  field_cat 'NAME3' 'ZTMMPR_APPROVE' ''  '' '' 'NAME3' 'ผู้ขาย (ต่อ2)' 'X' '10'.
  field_cat 'NAME4' 'ZTMMPR_APPROVE' ''  '' '' 'NAME4' 'ผู้ขาย (ต่อ3)' 'X' '10'.
  field_cat 'AMT'   'ZTMMPR_APPROVE' ''  '' '' 'AMT'   'มูลค่ารวม' 'X' ''.

ENDFORM.                    " PF_FIELD_CAT

*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       FORM pf_eventtab_build                                        *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       ........                                                      *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  TB_EVENTS                                                     *
*---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_eventtab_build USING tb_events TYPE slis_t_event.

  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <events>  LIKE LINE OF tb_events.
  CALL FUNCTION 'REUSE_ALV_EVENTS_GET'
    IMPORTING
      et_events       = tb_events
    EXCEPTIONS
      list_type_wrong = 1
      OTHERS          = 2.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    LOOP AT tb_events ASSIGNING <events>.
      CASE <events>-name.
        WHEN 'TOP_OF_PAGE'.
          <events>-form = 'PF_TOP_OF_PAGE'.
          EXIT.
      ENDCASE.
    ENDLOOP.
    DELETE tb_events WHERE form IS INITIAL.
  ELSE.
    MESSAGE ID sy-msgid TYPE sy-msgty NUMBER sy-msgno
            WITH sy-msgv1 sy-msgv2 sy-msgv3 sy-msgv4.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " PF_EVENTTAB_BUILD
