*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_CALCULATE_HEADER
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_calculate_header .
  DATA: ls_detail LIKE gt_detail,
        l_amt     LIKE bseg-dmbtr,
        l_cnt     TYPE i.

*--->Calculate line and summary
  LOOP AT gt_detail.
    MOVE gt_detail TO ls_detail.
    AT NEW banfn.
      CLEAR: l_amt,
             l_cnt.
    ENDAT.
*    L_AMT = L_AMT + ( LS_DETAIL-MENGE * LS_DETAIL-PREIS ). ">>> CHG-01:DELETE
*>>> CHG-01 : Start of insertion
    l_amt = l_amt + ( gt_detail-preis / gt_detail-peinh ) * gt_detail-menge.                        "<<< CHG-01:INSERT
*<<< CHG-01 : End of insertion
    l_cnt = l_cnt + 1.
    AT END OF banfn.
      LOOP AT gt_header
        WHERE banfn = ls_detail-banfn.
        gt_header-cnt = l_cnt.
        gt_header-sum = l_amt.
        MODIFY gt_header FROM gt_header.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDAT.

    gt_detail-seq = 1.
    MODIFY gt_detail.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " PF_CALCULATE_HEADER

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  pf_chk_diff_fund
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_chk_diff_fund .
  DATA: lt_accass LIKE gt_accass OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.
  DATA: l_chk(42),
        l_cur(42),
        l_pre(42),
        l_index TYPE i.

  lt_accass[] = gt_accass[].
  gt_tmp[]    = gt_detail[].
  SORT lt_accass BY banfn bnfpo geber fkber fistl.

  LOOP AT gt_header.
    CLEAR l_index.

    CASE gt_header-knttp.

      WHEN ' '. "Data keep in EBAN
        LOOP AT gt_detail
          WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn
          AND seq = 1.

          l_index = l_index + 1.
          CONCATENATE gt_detail-geber
                      gt_detail-fkber
                      gt_detail-fistl
                   INTO l_chk.
*--->Check differance fund
          IF l_index = 1.
            l_pre = l_chk.
            l_cur = l_chk.
          ELSE.
            l_pre = l_cur.
            l_cur = l_chk.
          ENDIF.
          IF l_pre <> l_cur.
            gt_header-no_hd = 'X'.
            MODIFY gt_header.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.

      WHEN OTHERS. "Data keep in EBKN
        LOOP AT lt_accass
          WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn.

          l_index = l_index + 1.
          CONCATENATE lt_accass-geber
                      lt_accass-fkber
                      lt_accass-fistl
                   INTO l_chk.
*--->Check differance fund
          IF l_index = 1.
            l_pre = l_chk.
            l_cur = l_chk.
          ELSE.
            l_pre = l_cur.
            l_cur = l_chk.
          ENDIF.
          IF l_pre <> l_cur.
            gt_header-no_hd = 'X'.
            MODIFY gt_header.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.                    " pf_chk_diff_fund

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  pf_fillin_result
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_fillin_result USING VALUE(us_type) TYPE c
                            VALUE(us_banfn) TYPE eban-banfn.
  DATA: ls_result LIKE gt_result,
        l_count   TYPE i.
  DATA: l_value1 TYPE string,
        l_value2 TYPE string,
        l_index  TYPE c.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <fs_data1> TYPE any,
                 <fs_data2> TYPE any.
  CLEAR: gt_result,
        l_count.
  CASE us_type.
    WHEN '0' OR '6'.
      gt_result-banfn = us_banfn.
      gt_result-txt1 = 'วงเงินตั้งแต่ 2,000,000 บาท'.
      APPEND gt_result.
    WHEN '1'.
      gt_result-banfn = us_banfn.
      gt_result-txt1 = 'วงเงินไม่เกิน 300,000 บาท'.
      APPEND gt_result.
    WHEN '2'.
      gt_result-banfn = us_banfn.
      gt_result-txt1 = 'วงเงินเกิน 300,000 บาท  แต่ไม่เกิน 2,000,000 บาท'.
      APPEND gt_result.
    WHEN '3'.
      gt_result-banfn = us_banfn.
      gt_result-txt1 = 'วงเงินเกิน 2,000,000 บาท'.
      APPEND gt_result.
    WHEN OTHERS.
*Parameter input
      DO 3  TIMES.
        MOVE sy-index TO l_index.
        CONCATENATE 'P_R' l_index INTO l_value1.
        UNASSIGN <fs_data1>.
        ASSIGN (l_value1) TO <fs_data1>.
        CHECK NOT <fs_data1> IS INITIAL.
*Line input
        DO 2  TIMES.
          MOVE  sy-index TO l_index.
          CONCATENATE l_value1 l_index INTO l_value2.
          UNASSIGN <fs_data2>.
          ASSIGN (l_value2) TO <fs_data2>.
          CHECK NOT <fs_data2> IS INITIAL.
          gt_result-banfn = us_banfn.
          gt_result-param = l_value1.
          gt_result-result = <fs_data1>.
          gt_result-txt1 = <fs_data2>.
          APPEND gt_result.
          l_count = l_count + 1.
        ENDDO.
      ENDDO.
*Concatenate word result
      LOOP AT gt_result
        WHERE banfn = us_banfn
          AND param <> ' '.
        MOVE gt_result TO ls_result.
        l_count = l_count - 1.
        AT END OF param.
          IF l_count > 0.
            CONCATENATE ls_result-txt1 ls_result-result 'และ'
                    INTO ls_result-txt1.
            MODIFY gt_result FROM ls_result.
          ELSE.
            CONCATENATE ls_result-txt1 ls_result-result
                     INTO ls_result-txt1.
            MODIFY gt_result FROM ls_result.
          ENDIF.
        ENDAT.
      ENDLOOP.
  ENDCASE.
ENDFORM.                    " pf_fillin_result

  IF p_r1 NE space.

    DELETE FROM ztmmreason .
    INSERT ztmmreason FROM *ztmmreason.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " SAVE_LAST_ACCESS

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

FORM pf_enhnacement_get_data TABLES it_tmp_pr it_tmp_exkn get_txt.
  DATA: lt_t0241 TYPE TABLE OF t024 WITH HEADER LINE.
  DATA: wa_tmp_pr   TYPE mereq_item,
        wa_tmp_exkn TYPE exkn.

  DATA: cnt_r TYPE i.
  "<<<CHG-06: Start Insert
*  DATA l_loop TYPE i VALUE 0.
*  LOOP AT gt_detail.
*    l_loop = 0.
*    WHILE l_loop = 0.
*      REPLACE '<(>&<)>' LENGTH 7 WITH '&' INTO gt_detail-txz01.
*      l_loop = sy-subrc.
*      MODIFY gt_detail.
*    ENDWHILE.
*  ENDLOOP.
  "<<<CHG-06: End Insert
  get_txt1[] = get_txt[].
  it_exkn[]  = it_tmp_exkn[].
  cnt_r = 0.
  LOOP AT it_tmp_pr INTO wa_tmp_pr.
    cnt_r = cnt_r + 1.
    IF cnt_r = 1.
*--->Get PR header
      IF wa_tmp_pr-ref_banfn NE space.
        gt_header-banfn = wa_tmp_pr-ref_banfn.
      ELSE.
        gt_header-banfn = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      v_banfn = gt_header-banfn .
      gt_header-bsart = wa_tmp_pr-bsart.
      gt_header-ekgrp = wa_tmp_pr-ekgrp.
      gt_header-knttp = wa_tmp_pr-knttp.
      gt_header-badat = wa_tmp_pr-badat.
      SELECT *
       INTO TABLE lt_t0241
       FROM t024
       FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_header
       WHERE ekgrp = gt_header-ekgrp.
      READ TABLE lt_t0241 WITH KEY ekgrp = gt_header-ekgrp.

      CLEAR: ebkn,fmfctrt,j_1bbranch,adrc.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM eban
      WHERE  banfn = gt_header-banfn
        AND  fistl NE space.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM fmfctrt
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
        AND fictr = eban-fistl.
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM j_1bbranch
      WHERE branch =   gt_header-bsart+1(2).
      SELECT SINGLE * FROM adrc
      WHERE  addrnumber = j_1bbranch-adrnr.

      CONCATENATE fmfctrt-beschr  adrc-name1
      adrc-name2
      INTO gt_header-addr SEPARATED BY space.
*      gt_header-addr = lt_t0241-smtp_addr.

      IF lt_t0241-tel_extens IS INITIAL.
        gt_header-tel = lt_t0241-tel_number.
      ELSE.
        CONCATENATE lt_t0241-tel_number '#' lt_t0241-tel_extens
        INTO gt_header-tel.
      ENDIF.
      gt_header-fax = lt_t0241-telfx.

      APPEND gt_header.

    ENDIF.
*--->Get PR detail
    IF wa_tmp_pr-ref_banfn NE space.
      gt_detail-banfn = wa_tmp_pr-ref_banfn.
    ELSE.
      gt_detail-banfn = '0'.
    ENDIF.

    gt_detail-bnfpo = wa_tmp_pr-bnfpo.
    gt_detail-matnr = wa_tmp_pr-matnr.
    gt_detail-txz01 = wa_tmp_pr-txz01.
    gt_detail-menge = wa_tmp_pr-menge.
    gt_detail-meins = wa_tmp_pr-meins.
    gt_detail-preis = wa_tmp_pr-preis.
    gt_detail-peinh = wa_tmp_pr-peinh.
    gt_detail-ebeln = wa_tmp_pr-ebeln.
    gt_detail-ebelp = wa_tmp_pr-ebelp.
* gt_detail-netpr = wa_tmp_pr-netpr.
    gt_detail-geber = wa_tmp_pr-geber.
    gt_detail-fkber = wa_tmp_pr-fkber.
    gt_detail-fistl = wa_tmp_pr-fistl.
    gt_detail-kblnr = wa_tmp_pr-kblnr.
    gt_detail-kblpos = wa_tmp_pr-kblpos.
    gt_detail-afnam = wa_tmp_pr-afnam.
    gt_detail-badat = wa_tmp_pr-badat.
    gt_detail-knttp = wa_tmp_pr-knttp.
*    gt_detail-werks = wa_tmp_pr-werks.                                      "INS CHG-25
    APPEND gt_detail.

*<<< BOI CHG-25
    CLEAR gt_text.
    MOVE: wa_tmp_pr-ref_banfn TO gt_text-banfn.

    SELECT SINGLE zdesc_im
      FROM ztmmdepartment

  ENDLOOP.
  c1 = 'X'.
  IF NOT gt_detail[] IS INITIAL.
    PERFORM pf_calculate_header.
    PERFORM pf_fillin_text1.
*    IF g_flag = 'Y'.
    PERFORM pf_get_text1.
    PERFORM pf_get_accass1.
    PERFORM pf_chk_diff_fund1.
    PERFORM pf_set_type_report.
    PERFORM pf_fillin_committee.
*--->Case show detail report
    IF c1 = 'X'.
      PERFORM pf_fillin_detail1.
    ENDIF.
*      PERFORM display_approve.
    PERFORM pf_print_doc1.
*    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
*  im_header[] = gt_header[].
*  im_detail[] = gt_detail[].
*  im_detail1[] = gt_detail1[].
*  im_text[] = gt_text[].
*  im_accass[] = gt_accass[].
*  im_comt[] = gt_comt[].
*  im_result[] = gt_result[].
  REFRESH:gt_header,gt_detail,gt_detail1,
          gt_text,gt_accass,gt_comt,gt_result.
  REFRESH:it_tmp_pr, it_tmp_exkn, get_txt.
ENDFORM.                    "pf_smart_get_data
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_FILLIN_TEXT1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_fillin_text1 .
  DATA: lt_line TYPE TABLE OF tline WITH HEADER LINE.
  DATA: l_obj  TYPE thead-tdname,
        l_line TYPE tline-tdline.


*--->Get text first line item is active
  gt_tmp[] = gt_detail[].

  SORT gt_tmp BY banfn bnfpo.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM gt_tmp
                   COMPARING banfn.
  LOOP AT gt_tmp.
    CLEAR gt_text.
    MOVE: gt_tmp-banfn TO gt_text-banfn,
          gt_tmp-bnfpo TO gt_text-bnfpo.

    CONCATENATE gt_tmp-banfn gt_tmp-bnfpo
           INTO l_obj.


    CLEAR l_line.
    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                          USING l_obj 'B05' 'EBAN' ' '.
*    IF LT_LINE[] IS INITIAL.
*      G_FLAG = 'N'.
*      EXIT.
*    ELSE.
    IF NOT lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
      LOOP AT lt_line
        WHERE tdline <> ' '.
        CONCATENATE l_line lt_line-tdline INTO l_line.
      ENDLOOP.
      CONDENSE l_line.
      MOVE: 'B05' TO gt_text-type,
            l_line TO gt_text-txt1.
      APPEND gt_text.
      CLEAR gt_text-txt1.
    ENDIF.


    CLEAR l_line.
    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                             USING l_obj 'B07' 'EBAN' ' '.
    IF lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
      g_flag = 'N'.
      EXIT.
    ELSE.

      LOOP AT lt_line
        WHERE tdline <> ' '.
        MOVE: 'B07' TO gt_text-type,
              lt_line-tdline TO gt_text-txt2.
        APPEND gt_text.
        CLEAR gt_text-txt2.
      ENDLOOP.

    ENDIF.

    CLEAR l_line.
    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                             USING l_obj 'B08' 'EBAN' ' '.
    IF lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
      g_flag = 'N'.
      EXIT.
    ELSE.
      LOOP AT lt_line
        WHERE tdline <> ' '.
        CONCATENATE l_line lt_line-tdline INTO l_line.
      ENDLOOP.
      CONDENSE l_line.
      MOVE: 'B08' TO gt_text-type,
            l_line TO gt_text-txt3.
      APPEND gt_text.
      CLEAR gt_text-txt3.
    ENDIF.

    CLEAR l_line.
    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
                             USING l_obj 'B09' 'EBAN' ' '.
    IF lt_line[] IS INITIAL.
      g_flag = 'N'.
      EXIT.
    ELSE.
      LOOP AT lt_line
        WHERE tdline <> ' '.
        CONCATENATE l_line lt_line-tdline INTO l_line.
      ENDLOOP.
      CONDENSE l_line.
      MOVE: 'B09' TO gt_text-type,
            l_line TO gt_text-txt4.
      APPEND gt_text.
*      CLEAR gt_text-txt4.
      CLEAR gt_text.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.                    " PF_FILLIN_TEXT1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  pf_get_text1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_get_text1 .
  DATA: lt_line TYPE TABLE OF tline WITH HEADER LINE.
  DATA: l_obj     TYPE thead-tdname,
        ls_detail LIKE gt_detail,
        l_index   LIKE sy-tabix,
        l_seq     TYPE i.
  DATA: lc_infnr TYPE eina-infnr.       "CHG-21: Insert
*--->Reprocess for get item text
  LOOP AT gt_detail
    WHERE menge <> 0.
    CLEAR l_seq.
    l_seq = 1.
    l_index = sy-tabix.
*    CONCATENATE gt_detail-banfn gt_detail-bnfpo
*           INTO l_obj.

    MOVE: gt_detail-banfn TO ls_detail-banfn,
          gt_detail-bnfpo TO ls_detail-bnfpo.


    CLEAR lt_line.
    v_fieldcount = 1.
    CLEAR ls_detail.

*---g_b05
    READ TABLE get_txt1 INTO wa_smb01 WITH KEY bxx = '05'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      g_b05 = wa_smb01-tdline.
    ENDIF.

*    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
*                             USING l_obj 'B01' 'EBAN' ' '.
* Start of comment: CHG-12
*    LOOP AT get_txt1 INTO wa_smb01
*        WHERE tdline <> ' ' AND bnfpo = gt_detail-bnfpo
*          AND bxx = '01'.
* End of comment: CHG-12
* Start of insert: CHG-12
    DATA: lt_get_txt  TYPE TABLE OF ty_txt1.
    FREE lt_get_txt.
    lt_get_txt[] = get_txt1[].
    DELETE lt_get_txt WHERE: bnfpo NE gt_detail-bnfpo,
                             bxx NE '01'.
    LOOP AT lt_get_txt INTO wa_smb01.
* End of insert: CHG-12

      IF wa_smb01-tdformat = '*'.
*        IF ls_detail-txz01 NE space.                             "CHG-12
        IF ls_detail-banfn NE space.                              "CHG-12
          APPEND ls_detail TO gt_detail.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR ls_detail.
        MOVE: gt_detail-banfn TO ls_detail-banfn,
        gt_detail-bnfpo TO ls_detail-bnfpo.
        v_fieldcount = 1.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail-seq = l_seq.
      ENDIF.

      CASE v_fieldcount.
        WHEN 1.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz01.
        WHEN 2.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz02.
        WHEN 3.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz03.
        WHEN 4.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz04.
        WHEN 5.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz05.
        WHEN 6.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz06.
        WHEN 7.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz07.
        WHEN 8.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz08.

      ENDCASE.

      v_fieldcount = v_fieldcount + 1.
      AT LAST.
        APPEND ls_detail TO gt_detail.
        CLEAR ls_detail.
        MOVE: gt_detail-banfn TO ls_detail-banfn,
      gt_detail-bnfpo TO ls_detail-bnfpo.
        v_fieldcount = 1.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail-seq = l_seq.
      ENDAT.


    ENDLOOP.


    CLEAR lt_line.
    v_fieldcount = 1.
    CLEAR ls_detail.

*    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
*                             USING l_obj 'B04' 'EBAN' ' '.

* Start of comment: CHG-12
*    LOOP AT get_txt1 INTO wa_smb01
*        WHERE tdline <> ' ' AND bnfpo = gt_detail-bnfpo
*          AND bxx = '04'.
* End of comment: CHG-12
* Start of insert: CHG-12
    FREE lt_get_txt.
    lt_get_txt[] = get_txt1[].
    DELETE lt_get_txt WHERE: bnfpo NE gt_detail-bnfpo,
                             bxx NE '04'.
    LOOP AT lt_get_txt INTO wa_smb01.
* End of insert: CHG-12
      IF wa_smb01-tdformat = '*'.
*        IF ls_detail-txz01 NE space.                             "CHG-12
        IF ls_detail-banfn IS NOT INITIAL.                        "CHG-12
          APPEND ls_detail TO gt_detail.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR ls_detail.
        MOVE: gt_detail-banfn TO ls_detail-banfn,
        gt_detail-bnfpo TO ls_detail-bnfpo.
        v_fieldcount = 1.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail-seq = l_seq.
      ENDIF.

      CASE v_fieldcount.
        WHEN 1.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz01.
        WHEN 2.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz02.
        WHEN 3.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz03.
        WHEN 4.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz04.
        WHEN 5.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz05.
        WHEN 6.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz06.
        WHEN 7.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz07.
        WHEN 8.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz08.

      ENDCASE.

      v_fieldcount = v_fieldcount + 1.
      AT LAST.
        APPEND ls_detail TO gt_detail.
        CLEAR ls_detail.
        MOVE: gt_detail-banfn TO ls_detail-banfn,
      gt_detail-bnfpo TO ls_detail-bnfpo.
        v_fieldcount = 1.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail-seq = l_seq.
      ENDAT.


    ENDLOOP.


    CLEAR: lt_line,v_fieldfirst.
    v_fieldcount = 1.
    CLEAR ls_detail.

*    PERFORM pf_read_text TABLES lt_line
*                             USING l_obj 'B02' 'EBAN' ' '.

* Start of comment: CHG-12
*    LOOP AT get_txt1 INTO wa_smb01
*        WHERE tdline <> ' ' AND bnfpo = gt_detail-bnfpo
*          AND bxx = '02'.
* End of comment: CHG-12
* Start of insert: CHG-12
    FREE lt_get_txt.
    lt_get_txt[] = get_txt1[].
    DELETE lt_get_txt WHERE: bnfpo NE gt_detail-bnfpo,
                             bxx NE '02'.
    LOOP AT lt_get_txt INTO wa_smb01.
* End of insert: CHG-12

      IF wa_smb01-tdformat = '*'.
*        IF ls_detail-txz01 NE space.                             "CHG-12
        IF ls_detail-banfn IS NOT INITIAL.                        "CHG-12
          APPEND ls_detail TO gt_detail.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR ls_detail.
        MOVE: gt_detail-banfn TO ls_detail-banfn,
        gt_detail-bnfpo TO ls_detail-bnfpo.
        v_fieldcount = 1.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail-seq = l_seq.
      ENDIF.


      ls_detail-peinh = '9'.
      CASE v_fieldcount.
        WHEN 1.
          IF v_fieldfirst = 0.
            CONCATENATE 'หมายเหตุรายการ:' wa_smb01-tdline "CH11
            INTO ls_detail-txz01
            SEPARATED BY space.
            v_fieldfirst = 1.
          ELSE.
            ls_detail-txz01 =  wa_smb01-tdline.
          ENDIF.


        WHEN 2.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz02.
        WHEN 3.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz03.
        WHEN 4.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz04.
        WHEN 5.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz05.
        WHEN 6.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz06.
        WHEN 7.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz07.
        WHEN 8.
          MOVE wa_smb01-tdline TO ls_detail-txz08.

      ENDCASE.

      v_fieldcount = v_fieldcount + 1.
      AT LAST.
        APPEND ls_detail TO gt_detail.
        CLEAR ls_detail.
        MOVE: gt_detail-banfn TO ls_detail-banfn,
      gt_detail-bnfpo TO ls_detail-bnfpo.
        v_fieldcount = 1.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail-seq = l_seq.
      ENDAT.


    ENDLOOP.
*CH11-->


*>>> CHG-01 : Start of deletion
*    GT_DETAIL-DMBTR = GT_DETAIL-MENGE * GT_DETAIL-PREIS.
*<<< CHG-01 : End of deletion
*>>> CHG-01 : Start of insertion
    gt_detail-dmbtr = ( gt_detail-preis / gt_detail-peinh ) * gt_detail-menge.
    gt_detail-preis = gt_detail-preis / gt_detail-peinh.
*    gt_detail-netpr = gt_detail-netpr / gt_detail-peinh.

    CLEAR gt_detail-netpr.

    ls_comim-matnr = gt_detail-matnr.
*    ls_comim-werks = gt_detail-werks. "CHG-18 ADD  "CHG-21: Del
    ls_comim-bqpra = 'X'.
***************************CHG-21: Insert Begin
    SELECT MAX( infnr )
      FROM eina
      INTO lc_infnr
      WHERE matnr = gt_detail-matnr.
    SELECT werks erdat
      FROM eine
      INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_chkwerks
      WHERE infnr = lc_infnr.
    SORT it_chkwerks BY erdat DESCENDING.
    READ TABLE it_chkwerks INTO wa_chkwerks INDEX 1.
    ls_comim-werks = wa_chkwerks-werks.
***************************CHG-21: Insert END
    CALL FUNCTION 'ME_SEARCH_SOURCE_OF_SUPPLY_EXP'
      EXPORTING
        if_preq_no  = gt_detail-banfn
        if_preq_pos = gt_detail-bnfpo
        is_comim    = ls_comim
      TABLES
        et_src_tab  = itab_src_tab.

    IF NOT itab_src_tab[] IS INITIAL.
      DESCRIBE TABLE itab_src_tab LINES v_lines.
      READ TABLE itab_src_tab INTO wa_et_src_tab INDEX v_lines.
      gt_detail-netpr = wa_et_src_tab-netpr.
    ENDIF.
*<<< CHG-01 : End of insertion

    MODIFY gt_detail INDEX l_index.
  ENDLOOP.
*---Sort itab
  SORT gt_detail BY banfn bnfpo seq.

ENDFORM.                    " pf_get_text1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_GET_ACCASS1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_get_accass1 .
  DATA: lt_fmfint  TYPE TABLE OF fmfint WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_tfkbt   TYPE TABLE OF tfkbt WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_fmfctrt TYPE TABLE OF fmfctrt WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_skat    TYPE TABLE OF skat WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_aufk    TYPE TABLE OF aufk WITH HEADER LINE,
        lt_cskt    TYPE TABLE OF cskt WITH HEADER LINE.

  DATA: l_p1   TYPE setnode-setname,
        l_des1 TYPE setheadert-descript,
        l_p2   TYPE setnode-setname,
        l_des2 TYPE setheadert-descript.
*--->Get account assignment

  LOOP AT it_exkn INTO wa_exkn.
    gt_accass-banfn  =  v_banfn.
    gt_accass-bnfpo  =  wa_exkn-sempsl.
*       gt_accass-zebkn  =  wa_exkn-zebkn.
    gt_accass-sakto  =  wa_exkn-sakto.
    gt_accass-kostl  =  wa_exkn-kostl.
    gt_accass-geber  =  wa_exkn-geber.
    gt_accass-fkber  =  wa_exkn-fkber.
    gt_accass-fistl  =  wa_exkn-fistl.
    gt_accass-kblnr  =  wa_exkn-kblnr.
    gt_accass-kblpos =  wa_exkn-kblpos.
    gt_accass-anln1  =  wa_exkn-anln1.
    gt_accass-anln2  =  wa_exkn-anln2.
    gt_accass-menge  =  wa_exkn-menge.
    gt_accass-aufnr  =  wa_exkn-aufnr.
    APPEND gt_accass.
  ENDLOOP.
  IF NOT gt_accass[] IS INITIAL.
*--->Get description
    SELECT *
      FROM fmfint
      INTO TABLE lt_fmfint
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
       AND  fincode = gt_accass-geber.

    SELECT *
      FROM tfkbt
      INTO TABLE lt_tfkbt
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
       AND  fkber = gt_accass-fkber.

    SELECT *
      FROM fmfctrt
      INTO TABLE lt_fmfctrt
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
       AND  fictr = gt_accass-fistl.

    SELECT *
      FROM skat
      INTO TABLE lt_skat
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
        AND ktopl = '1000'
        AND saknr = gt_accass-sakto.

    SELECT *
      FROM cskt
      INTO TABLE lt_cskt
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE spras = sy-langu
        AND kokrs = '1000'
        AND kostl = gt_accass-kostl.

    SELECT *
      FROM aufk
      INTO TABLE lt_aufk
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_accass
      WHERE aufnr = gt_accass-aufnr.

    LOOP AT gt_accass.
      READ TABLE lt_fmfint WITH KEY fincode = gt_accass-geber.
      gt_accass-ge_des = lt_fmfint-beschr.

      READ TABLE lt_tfkbt WITH KEY fkber = gt_accass-fkber.
      gt_accass-fk_des = lt_tfkbt-fkbtx.

      READ TABLE lt_fmfctrt WITH KEY fictr = gt_accass-fistl.
      gt_accass-fi_des = lt_fmfctrt-beschr.

      READ TABLE lt_skat WITH KEY saknr = gt_accass-sakto.
      gt_accass-sa_des = lt_skat-txt50.

      READ TABLE lt_cskt WITH KEY kostl = gt_accass-kostl.
      gt_accass-ko_des = lt_cskt-ltext.

      READ TABLE lt_aufk WITH KEY aufnr = gt_accass-aufnr.
      gt_accass-ktext = lt_aufk-ktext.

*--->แผนหลัก และ แผนงาน
      CLEAR: l_p1, l_des1, l_p2, l_des2.
      CALL FUNCTION 'ZMM_FA_DESCRIPTION'
        EXPORTING
          key       = gt_accass-fkber
        IMPORTING
          p1        = l_p1
          des1      = l_des1
          p2        = l_p2
          des2      = l_des2
        EXCEPTIONS
          not_found = 1
          OTHERS    = 2.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gt_accass-plan1 = l_p1.
        gt_accass-p1_des = l_des1.
        gt_accass-plan2 = l_p2.
        gt_accass-p2_des = l_des2.
      ENDIF.

      MODIFY gt_accass.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

*--->Fill in description account assignment case data in EBAN
  READ TABLE gt_header WITH KEY knttp = ' '.
  CHECK sy-subrc = 0.
*--->Get description
  SELECT *
    FROM fmfint
    INTO TABLE lt_fmfint
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
    WHERE spras = sy-langu
     AND  fincode = gt_detail-geber.

  SELECT *
    FROM tfkbt
    INTO TABLE lt_tfkbt
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
    WHERE spras = sy-langu
     AND  fkber = gt_detail-fkber.

  SELECT *
    FROM fmfctrt
    INTO TABLE lt_fmfctrt
    FOR ALL ENTRIES IN gt_detail
    WHERE spras = sy-langu
     AND  fictr = gt_detail-fistl.

  LOOP AT gt_header
    WHERE knttp = ' '.
    LOOP AT gt_detail
      WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn
        AND seq = '1'.
      CLEAR gt_accass.
      MOVE: gt_detail-banfn TO gt_accass-banfn,
            gt_detail-bnfpo TO gt_accass-bnfpo,
            gt_detail-geber TO gt_accass-geber,
            gt_detail-fkber TO gt_accass-fkber,
            gt_detail-fistl TO gt_accass-fistl.

      READ TABLE lt_fmfint WITH KEY fincode = gt_accass-geber.
      gt_accass-ge_des = lt_fmfint-beschr.

      READ TABLE lt_tfkbt WITH KEY fkber = gt_accass-fkber.
      gt_accass-fk_des = lt_tfkbt-fkbtx.

      READ TABLE lt_fmfctrt WITH KEY fictr = gt_accass-fistl.
      gt_accass-fi_des = lt_fmfctrt-beschr.

*--->แผนหลัก และ แผนงาน
      CLEAR: l_p1, l_des1, l_p2, l_des2.
      CALL FUNCTION 'ZMM_FA_DESCRIPTION'
        EXPORTING
          key       = gt_accass-fkber
        IMPORTING
          p1        = l_p1
          des1      = l_des1
          p2        = l_p2
          des2      = l_des2
        EXCEPTIONS
          not_found = 1
          OTHERS    = 2.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        gt_accass-plan1 = l_p1.
        gt_accass-p1_des = l_des1.
        gt_accass-plan2 = l_p2.
        gt_accass-p2_des = l_des2.
      ENDIF.

      APPEND gt_accass.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDLOOP.
*Sort data
*  SORT gt_accass BY banfn bnfpo zebkn.
ENDFORM.                    " PF_GET_ACCASS1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  pf_chk_diff_fund
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_chk_diff_fund1 .
  DATA: lt_accass LIKE gt_accass OCCURS 0 WITH HEADER LINE.
  DATA: l_chk(42),
        l_cur(42),
        l_pre(42),
        l_index TYPE i.

  lt_accass[] = gt_accass[].
  gt_tmp[]    = gt_detail[].
*  SORT lt_accass BY banfn bnfpo geber fkber fistl.

  LOOP AT gt_header.
    CLEAR l_index.

    CASE gt_header-knttp.

      WHEN ' '. "Data keep in EBAN
        LOOP AT gt_detail
          WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn
          AND seq = 1.

          l_index = l_index + 1.
          CONCATENATE gt_detail-geber
                      gt_detail-fkber
                      gt_detail-fistl
                   INTO l_chk.
*--->Check differance fund
          IF l_index = 1.
            l_pre = l_chk.
            l_cur = l_chk.
          ELSE.
            l_pre = l_cur.
            l_cur = l_chk.
          ENDIF.
          IF l_pre <> l_cur.
            gt_header-no_hd = 'X'.
            MODIFY gt_header.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.

      WHEN OTHERS. "Data keep in EBKN
        LOOP AT lt_accass
          WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn.

          l_index = l_index + 1.
          CONCATENATE lt_accass-geber
                      lt_accass-fkber
                      lt_accass-fistl
                   INTO l_chk.
*--->Check differance fund
          IF l_index = 1.
            l_pre = l_chk.
            l_cur = l_chk.
          ELSE.
            l_pre = l_cur.
            l_cur = l_chk.
          ENDIF.
          IF l_pre <> l_cur.
            gt_header-no_hd = 'X'.
            MODIFY gt_header.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDLOOP.
ENDFORM.                    " pf_chk_diff_fund1

*&      Form  PF_FILLIN_DETAIL1

*  <--  p2        text

FORM pf_fillin_detail1 .
  DATA: lt_line TYPE TABLE OF tline WITH HEADER LINE.

  DATA: ls_detail1 LIKE gt_detail,
        l_seq      TYPE i,
        l_cnt      TYPE i.
  DATA: l_obj TYPE thead-tdname.
  DATA: lgt_accass LIKE  gt_accass. "

*--->Prepart itab for show detail
  LOOP AT gt_detail INTO ls_detail1.       "CHG-18 DEL    "CHG-21: Insert
*  LOOP AT gt_detail_tmp INTO ls_detail1.    "CHG-18 ADD  "CHG-21: Del
    IF ls_detail1-peinh NE '9'.
      APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
*  gt_detail1[] = gt_detail[].

  SORT gt_detail BY banfn bnfpo ASCENDING seq DESCENDING.      "CHG-18 DEL  "CHG-21: Insert
*  SORT gt_detail_tmp BY banfn bnfpo ASCENDING seq DESCENDING.  "CHG-18 ADD "CHG-21: Del

  LOOP AT gt_detail1
    WHERE seq = 1.

    CLEAR: ls_detail1, l_seq, l_cnt.
*Set PR, Item
    MOVE: gt_detail1-banfn TO ls_detail1-banfn,
          gt_detail1-bnfpo TO ls_detail1-bnfpo.

*Check last seq per docment
    READ TABLE gt_detail WITH KEY banfn = gt_detail1-banfn     "CHG-18 DEL  "CHG-21: Insert
                                  bnfpo = gt_detail1-bnfpo.    "CHG-18 DEL  "CHG-21: Insert
*    READ TABLE gt_detail_tmp WITH KEY banfn = gt_detail1-banfn  "CHG-18 ADD  "CHG-21: Del
*                                      bnfpo = gt_detail1-bnfpo. "CHG-18 ADD  "CHG-21: Del

*    l_seq = gt_detail_tmp-seq.    "CHG-18 ADD    "CHG-21: Del

*      CONCATENATE 'รหัสสินค้า:' gt_detail1-matnr
*              INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*              SEPARATED BY space.

      ls_detail1-txz01 = 'รหัสสินค้า:'.
*      ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_detail1-matnr.

        EXPORTING
          input  = gt_detail1-matnr
        IMPORTING
          output = ls_detail1-txz02.


*      ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-sa_des.

    ENDIF.

    IF gt_detail1-geber IS INITIAL.
*==============Case data in EBKN==============

        WHERE banfn = gt_detail1-banfn

          l_seq = l_seq + 1.
          ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
          APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
          EXIT.
        ELSE.
*G/L
          IF NOT gt_accass-sakto IS INITIAL.
            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'บัญชี:'.
            ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_accass-sakto.
            ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-sa_des.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.
*            CONCATENATE 'บัญชี:' gt_accass-sa_des
*                INTO ls_detail1-txz01
*                SEPARATED BY space.
**            MOVE gt_accass-menge TO ls_detail1-menge.
            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR ls_detail1-menge.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
                  ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
          ENDIF.
*ศูนย์ต้นทุน
          IF NOT gt_accass-kostl IS INITIAL.
            ls_detail1-txz01 = 'ศูนย์ต้นทุน:'.
            ls_detail1-txz02 = gt_accass-kostl.
            ls_detail1-txz03 = gt_accass-ko_des.
            ls_detail1-peinh = '8'.

*                SEPARATED BY space.
            l_seq = l_seq + 1.
            ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
            APPEND ls_detail1 TO gt_detail1.
            CLEAR:ls_detail1-txz01,ls_detail1-txz02,
                  ls_detail1-txz03 ,ls_detail1-peinh.
          ENDIF.
*รหัสสินทรัพย์
          IF NOT gt_accass-anln1 IS INITIAL.

            WHERE anln1 = gt_accass-anln1
              AND anln2 = gt_accass-anln2.                      "CHG-14

        v_fieldcount = 1.
        l_seq = l_seq + 1.
        ls_detail1-seq = l_seq.
      ENDAT.


    ENDLOOP.

  ENDLOOP.

  SORT gt_detail1 BY banfn bnfpo seq.
  SORT gt_detail BY banfn bnfpo seq.

FORM pf_print_doc1.
  DATA: func_mod_name1 TYPE rs38l_fnam,
        func_mod_name2 TYPE rs38l_fnam,
        func_mod_name3 TYPE rs38l_fnam,
        f_name         TYPE tdsfname,
        l_name1        TYPE string,
        l_name2        TYPE string,
        l_name3        TYPE string,
        l_posi1        TYPE string,
        l_posi2        TYPE string,
        l_posi3        TYPE string.
  DATA: ssfctrlop LIKE ssfctrlop,
        l_type    TYPE c.
  DATA : output TYPE ssfcompop. " For output Parameters

*--->Get Function Mode Name in System
*  f_name = 'ZLPUFM01_1_FORM'.          "<< INSERT FORM: art
*  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
*    EXPORTING
*      formname = f_name
*    IMPORTING

  f_name = 'ZLPUFM01_2_FORM_NEW'.          "<< INSERT FORM: art     "INS CHG-25
  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
    EXPORTING
      formname = f_name
    IMPORTING
      fm_name  = func_mod_name2.
*  f_name = 'ZLPUFM01_3_FORM'.          "<< INSERT FORM: art
*  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_FUNCTION_MODULE_NAME'
*    EXPORTING
*      formname = f_name
*    IMPORTING
*      fm_name  = func_mod_name3.

*--->Set name
*Name1
  IF NOT ( t10_txt1 IS INITIAL AND
           t10_txt3 IS INITIAL AND
           t10_txt4 IS INITIAL ).
    CONCATENATE '(' t10_txt1 t10_txt3 t10_txt4 ')' INTO l_name1
    SEPARATED BY space..
  ELSE.
    CONCATENATE '(.....................................'
                  '....................................)'
           INTO l_name1.
  ENDIF.
*Position1
  MOVE t10_txt2 TO l_posi1.

*Name2
  IF NOT ( t11_txt1 IS INITIAL AND
           t11_txt3 IS INITIAL AND
           t11_txt4 IS INITIAL ).
    CONCATENATE '(' t11_txt1 t11_txt3 t11_txt4 ')' INTO l_name2
    SEPARATED BY space..
  ELSE.
    CONCATENATE '(.....................................'
                '....................................)'
         INTO l_name2.
  ENDIF.
*Position2
  MOVE t11_txt2 TO l_posi2.

*Name3
  IF NOT ( t12_txt1 IS INITIAL AND
           t12_txt3 IS INITIAL AND
           t12_txt4 IS INITIAL ).
    CONCATENATE '(' t12_txt1 t12_txt3 t12_txt4 ')' INTO l_name3
    SEPARATED BY space.
  ELSE.
    CONCATENATE '(.....................................'
                '....................................)'
         INTO l_name3.
  ENDIF.
*Position3
  MOVE t12_txt2 TO l_posi3.

*--->Call Smartforms by Function Mode Name
*  IF c1 = ' ' AND c2 = ' '. "PR
*    l_type = '1'.
*  ELSEIF c1 = 'X' AND c2 = ' '. "PR, PR detail
*    l_type = '2'.
*  ELSEIF c1 = ' ' AND c2 = 'X'. "PR, Req Approve
*    l_type = '3'.
*  ELSE. "PR, PR Detail, Req Approve
*    l_type = '4'.
*  ENDIF.

*  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_OPEN'.
  ssfctrlop-no_open = 'X'.
  ssfctrlop-no_close = 'X'.
  ssfctrlop-device = 'PRINTER'.
  ssfctrlop-preview = 'X'.
  ssfctrlop-no_dialog = 'X'.


  output-tddest = 'LOCL'.
  output-tdimmed = 'X'.
  output-tdnoprint = 'X'.

*<<< BOI CHG-26
  IF p_fiori = abap_true.
    output-tddest = 'LPDF'.
  ENDIF.
*<<< EOI CHG-26

  CALL FUNCTION 'SSF_OPEN'
    EXPORTING
      output_options     = output
      control_parameters = ssfctrlop
      user_settings      = ''
    EXCEPTIONS
      formatting_error   = 1
      internal_error     = 2
      send_error         = 3
      user_canceled      = 4
      OTHERS             = 5.
  g_subj = 'XX'.
*  LOOP AT gt_header.
  READ TABLE gt_header INDEX 1.

  " clear GV_QRCODE to not display qr code in form    " +CHG-33
  CLEAR gv_qrcode.                                    " +CHG-33

  CALL FUNCTION func_mod_name2
    EXPORTING
      control_parameters = ssfctrlop

      user_settings      = ' '
      g_banfn            = gt_header-banfn

      g_dar              = p_sub

      p1                 = l_posi1
      n2                 = l_name2
      p2                 = l_posi2

      p3                 = l_posi3.
*   ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.                    " PF_PRINT_DOC1

FORM pf_chk_req_fld_type_2 .

  IF sy-ucomm = 'ONLI' OR
     sy-ucomm = 'CHK_OPEN'.
    EXIT.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR: itab_ztmmdoctypedesc, ztmmprcommittee.
  REFRESH itab_ztmmdoctypedesc.

  CLEAR eban.
*<<< BOE CHG-25

*    AND loekz = space.

    INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_eban_temp)

    AND loekz = @space.

  SORT lt_eban_temp BY bnfpo.
  READ TABLE lt_eban_temp INTO eban INDEX 1.
*>>> EOE CHG-25

  IF sy-subrc NE 0.
    MESSAGE e000(zcu) WITH 'ไม่พบข้อมูลใบขอเสนอซื้อ'(e03).
    RETURN.
  ENDIF.

  v_matkl = eban-matkl.
  gv_knttp = eban-knttp.

*>Begin ins by CHG-27
  IF p_r1 IS NOT INITIAL.

      FROM ztmmresult
      INTO @DATA(lw_ztmmres)
      WHERE zdata            = @p_r1
        AND zpr_method_range = @eban-bsart.
    IF sy-subrc IS NOT INITIAL.
      MESSAGE e000(zcu) WITH 'เหตุผลไม่สัมพันธ์กับประเภทเอกสาร'.
      RETURN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  WHERE banfn IN s_banfn.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    SELECT  * FROM ztmmdoctypedesc
    INTO TABLE itab_ztmmdoctypedesc
    WHERE pr_method EQ ztmmprcommittee-pr_method
      AND zcu_rule EQ ztmmprcommittee-zcu_rule.
  ELSE.
    SELECT  * FROM ztmmdoctypedesc
    INTO TABLE itab_ztmmdoctypedesc
    WHERE pr_method EQ eban-bsart+3(1)
*      AND zcu_rule EQ '1'. "CHG-17 DEL
* CHG-17 START INS
      AND zcu_rule EQ '2'.
    IF sy-subrc NE 0.
      SELECT  * FROM ztmmdoctypedesc
      INTO TABLE itab_ztmmdoctypedesc
      WHERE pr_method EQ eban-bsart+3(1)
        AND zcu_rule EQ '1'.
    ENDIF.
* CHG-17 END INS
  ENDIF.

  IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
    READ TABLE itab_ztmmdoctypedesc WITH KEY matkl = v_matkl.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      IF itab_ztmmdoctypedesc-zreason NE space.
        v_ztype =  itab_ztmmdoctypedesc-ztype.
        p_mr = itab_ztmmdoctypedesc-zreason.
      ENDIF.
    ELSE.
      READ TABLE itab_ztmmdoctypedesc WITH KEY matkl = space.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        IF itab_ztmmdoctypedesc-zreason NE space.
          v_ztype =  itab_ztmmdoctypedesc-ztype.
          p_mr = itab_ztmmdoctypedesc-zreason.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  CHECK p_mr IS INITIAL.

  LOOP AT SCREEN.
    IF screen-group1 = 'RE'.
      IF p_mr EQ space.
        screen-active  = '1'.
        "<<< CHG-24 Start Comment
*        CASE SCREEN-NAME.
*          WHEN 'P_R1'." OR 'P_R11'.
*            SCREEN-REQUIRED = '1'.
**          WHEN 'P_R11'.
**            IF p_r1 IS INITIAL.
**              screen-required = '0'.
**            ELSE.
**              screen-required = '1'.
**            ENDIF.
*        ENDCASE.
        ">>> CHG-24 End Comment
      ELSE.
        screen-active  = '0'.
      ENDIF.
      MODIFY SCREEN.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

*  CHECK editor2 IS BOUND.
*  IF eban-knttp = 'U'.
*
*    DATA: lt_lines TYPE TABLE OF tline,
*          lv_name  TYPE thead-tdname.
*
*    CONCATENATE eban-banfn eban-bnfpo INTO lv_name.

**       CLIENT                  = SY-MANDT

*        lines                   = lt_lines

*        id                      = 1
*        language                = 2
*        name                    = 3
*        not_found               = 4
*        object                  = 5
*        reference_check         = 6

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F4_ZTMMRESULT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f4_ztmmresult USING p_r1 p_r2 p_r3.
  DATA: lv_cursor_line TYPE sy-stepl,
        ls_dynpfields  TYPE dynpread,
        lt_dynpfields  TYPE TABLE OF dynpread,
        lt_return      TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ddshretval,
        ls_return      TYPE ddshretval,
        lwa_result     LIKE LINE OF gt_f4_result.

  GET CURSOR LINE lv_cursor_line.

  PERFORM f4_get_ztmmresult.

  SORT gt_f4_result BY zkey.
*brea
  CALL FUNCTION 'F4IF_INT_TABLE_VALUE_REQUEST'
    EXPORTING
      retfield        = 'ZKEY'
      dynpprog        = sy-repid
      dynpnr          = '1000'
      dynprofield     = 'ZLPUFM001'
      value_org       = 'S'
    TABLES
      value_tab       = gt_f4_result
      return_tab      = lt_return
    EXCEPTIONS
      parameter_error = 1
      no_values_found = 2
      OTHERS          = 3.

  IF sy-subrc = 0.

    READ TABLE lt_return INTO ls_return INDEX 1.
    READ TABLE gt_f4_result INTO lwa_result
    WITH KEY zkey = ls_return-fieldval.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      IF p_r1 EQ 'X'.
        ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue = lwa_result-zdata.
        ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_R1'.
        ls_dynpfields-stepl = lv_cursor_line.
        APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.

        ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_R11'.
        ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue = lwa_result-zldesc.
        ls_dynpfields-stepl = lv_cursor_line.
        APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.
      ELSEIF p_r2 EQ 'X'.
        ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue = lwa_result-zdata.
        ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_R2'.
        ls_dynpfields-stepl = lv_cursor_line.
        APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.

        ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_R21'.
        ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue = lwa_result-zldesc.
        ls_dynpfields-stepl = lv_cursor_line.
        APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.
      ELSEIF p_r3 EQ 'X'.
        ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue = lwa_result-zdata.
        ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_R3'.
        ls_dynpfields-stepl = lv_cursor_line.
        APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.

        ls_dynpfields-fieldname = 'P_R31'.
        ls_dynpfields-fieldvalue = lwa_result-zldesc.
        ls_dynpfields-stepl = lv_cursor_line.
        APPEND ls_dynpfields TO lt_dynpfields.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.

    CALL FUNCTION 'DYNP_VALUES_UPDATE'
      EXPORTING
        dyname     = 'ZLPUFM001'
        dynumb     = '1000'
      TABLES
        dynpfields = lt_dynpfields.

  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F4_ZTMMRESULT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  F4_GET_ZTMMRESULT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM f4_get_ztmmresult .
  TYPES: BEGIN OF lty_eban,
           banfn TYPE eban-banfn,
           bnfpo TYPE eban-bnfpo,
           loekz TYPE eban-loekz,
           bsart TYPE eban-bsart,
         END OF lty_eban.
  DATA: lwa_result  LIKE LINE OF gt_f4_result,
        lt_eban     TYPE TABLE OF lty_eban,
        lwa_eban    LIKE LINE OF lt_eban,
        lv_zcu_rule TYPE ztmmprcommittee-zcu_rule.
*  RANGES: lr_ranges FOR ztmmresult-zpr_method_range.
  REFRESH: gt_f4_result.
  IF s_banfn[] IS INITIAL.
    SELECT zkey zdata zdesc zldesc
    INTO TABLE gt_f4_result
    FROM ztmmresult.
  ELSE.
    IF r_ranges[] IS INITIAL.
      SELECT banfn bnfpo loekz bsart
      INTO TABLE lt_eban
      FROM eban
      WHERE banfn IN s_banfn
      AND   loekz EQ space.
* Append ranges for search help: P_R1, P_R2, P_R3
      LOOP AT lt_eban INTO lwa_eban.
        CLEAR r_ranges.
        r_ranges = 'ICP'.
        CONCATENATE '*' lwa_eban-bsart+3(1) '*'
        INTO r_ranges-low.
        APPEND r_ranges.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDIF.
    SELECT SINGLE zcu_rule INTO lv_zcu_rule
    FROM ztmmprcommittee
    WHERE banfn IN s_banfn.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      SELECT zkey zdata zdesc zldesc
      INTO TABLE gt_f4_result
      FROM ztmmresult
      WHERE zcu_rule EQ lv_zcu_rule
      AND   zpr_method_range IN r_ranges.
    ELSE.
      SELECT zkey zdata zdesc zldesc
      INTO TABLE gt_f4_result
      FROM ztmmresult
      WHERE zpr_method_range IN r_ranges.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " F4_GET_ZTMMRESULT

FORM validate_eban  CHANGING p_o_error.

  TYPES: BEGIN OF lty_eban,
           banfn TYPE eban-banfn,
           bnfpo TYPE eban-bnfpo,
           loekz TYPE eban-loekz,
           bsart TYPE eban-bsart,
         END OF lty_eban.
  DATA: lwa_result    LIKE LINE OF gt_f4_result,
        lt_eban       TYPE TABLE OF lty_eban,
*        lt_com        TYPE TABLE OF ztmmprcommittee,                       "DEL CHG-25
        lwa_eban      LIKE LINE OF lt_eban,
        lt_weight     TYPE TABLE OF ztmmprweight,
        lwa_weight    LIKE LINE OF lt_weight,
        lv_ranges(10) TYPE n,
        lv_zcu_rule   TYPE ztmmprcommittee-zcu_rule,
        lv_msg        TYPE char100.


  DATA: lv_check_rule       TYPE ztmmprweight-zcu_rule,
*        lwa_com_check       LIKE LINE OF lt_com,                           "DEL CHG-25
        lwa_com_check       LIKE LINE OF gt_com,                            "INS CHG-25
        lwa_weight_check    LIKE LINE OF lt_weight,
        lv_dont_validate(1).

  CLEAR p_o_error.
  REFRESH: r_ranges, gt_f4_result.
  IF p_mr IS INITIAL AND gv_chk_2017 IS NOT INITIAL. " CHG-24 add gv_chg_2017
    IF p_r11 IS INITIAL.
*   Error: no record found in ZTMMPRWEIGHT
      p_o_error = 'X'.
*      MESSAGE 'กรุณากรอกรายละเอียดของเหตุผล'  TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. "CHG-24 comment
      MESSAGE 'กรุณาระบุเหตุผลในการจัดหา'  TYPE 'S' DISPLAY LIKE 'E'. "CHG-24 insert
      EXIT.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  IF NOT s_banfn[] IS INITIAL.
    SELECT banfn bnfpo loekz bsart
    INTO TABLE lt_eban
    FROM eban
    WHERE banfn IN s_banfn
    AND   loekz EQ space.
    IF NOT lt_eban[] IS INITIAL.

      SELECT * INTO TABLE lt_weight
      FROM ztmmprweight
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_eban
      WHERE banfn EQ lt_eban-banfn.

*      SELECT * INTO TABLE lt_com                                                       "DEL CHG-25
      SELECT * INTO TABLE gt_com                                                        "INS CHG-25
      FROM ztmmprcommittee
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN lt_eban
      WHERE banfn EQ lt_eban-banfn
        AND ( committee_group EQ '06'
           OR committee_group EQ '11'                                                   "INS CHG-25
           OR committee_group EQ '18'                                                   "INS CHG-25
           OR committee_group EQ '19'                                                   "INS CHG-25
           OR committee_group EQ '20' ).            "<<<CHG-31: Insert
      "SORT gt_com BY item_no.       "INS CHG-25    "<<<CHG-31: Delete
      SORT gt_com BY banfn committee_group item_no. "<<<CHG-31: Insert
    ENDIF.

*> ADD
    DATA lv_range TYPE ztmmprcriteria-zpr_method_range.
    SELECT SINGLE zpr_method_range
      INTO lv_range
      FROM ztmmprcriteria
      WHERE zcu_rule = '2'.
    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      SELECT SINGLE zpr_method_range
        INTO lv_range
        FROM ztmmprcriteria
        WHERE zcu_rule = ' '.
      IF sy-subrc <> 0.
        SELECT SINGLE zpr_method_range
          INTO lv_range
          FROM ztmmprcriteria
          WHERE zcu_rule = '1'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
*< ADD


    LOOP AT lt_eban INTO lwa_eban.
      CLEAR: lv_check_rule, lv_dont_validate.

*      READ TABLE lt_com INTO lwa_com_check                                       "DEL CHG-25
      READ TABLE gt_com INTO lwa_com_check                                        "INS CHG-25
      WITH KEY banfn = lwa_eban-banfn.
      IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
        IF lwa_com_check-zcu_rule EQ '2'.
*> ADD
          IF lwa_eban-bsart+3(1) CA lv_range.
            "validate
            CLEAR lv_dont_validate.
          ELSE.
            lv_dont_validate = 'X'."no weight + no committee
          ENDIF.
*< ADD
        ELSE.
          lv_dont_validate = 'X'."no weight + no committee
        ENDIF.
      ELSE.
        READ TABLE lt_weight INTO lwa_weight_check
        WITH KEY banfn = lwa_eban-banfn.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
          IF lwa_weight_check-zcu_rule EQ '2'.
*> ADD
            IF lwa_eban-bsart+3(1) CA lv_range.
              "validate
              CLEAR lv_dont_validate.
            ELSE.
              lv_dont_validate = 'X'."no weight + no committee
            ENDIF.
*< ADD
          ELSE.
            lv_dont_validate = 'X'."no weight + no committee
          ENDIF.
        ELSE.
          lv_dont_validate = 'X'."no weight + no committee
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.

      IF lv_dont_validate EQ 'X'.

        IF lwa_eban-bsart+3(1) CA lv_range.
          "validate
          CLEAR lv_dont_validate.
        ENDIF.

*        CASE lwa_eban-bsart+3(1).
*          WHEN '0' OR '1' OR '2' OR '3' OR '4' OR
*               '5' OR '6' OR '7' OR '8' OR '9' OR 'L'.
**          "dont validate
*          WHEN 'A' OR 'B' OR 'C' OR 'D' OR 'E' OR
*               'F' OR 'G' OR 'H' OR 'I' OR 'J' OR
*               'K'." OR 'L'.
*            "validaet
*            CLEAR lv_dont_validate.
*          WHEN OTHERS.


*        CASE lwa_eban-bsart+3(1).
*          WHEN '0' OR '1' OR '2' OR '3' OR '4' OR
*               '5' OR '6' OR '7' OR '8' OR '9' OR 'L'.
**          "dont validate
*          WHEN 'A' OR 'B' OR 'C' OR 'D' OR 'E' OR
*               'F' OR 'G' OR 'H' OR 'I' OR 'J' OR
*               'K'." OR 'L'.
*            "validaet
*            CLEAR lv_dont_validate.
*          WHEN OTHERS.
        "dont validate
*    ENDCASE.
      ENDIF.

      IF lv_dont_validate EQ space.
*   Search header level
        READ TABLE lt_weight INTO lwa_weight
        WITH KEY banfn = lwa_eban-banfn
                 bnfpo = 0.
*   Header level found then there is no error.
        IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*   Header level is not found in ZTMMPRWEIGHT then search record by PR NO
        ELSE.
          READ TABLE lt_weight INTO lwa_weight
          WITH KEY banfn = lwa_eban-banfn.
*   Record saved in ZTMMPRWEIGHT
          IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
*   Case item: BNFPO is not initial
            IF NOT lwa_weight-bnfpo IS INITIAL. "Case item
              READ TABLE lt_weight INTO lwa_weight
              WITH KEY banfn = lwa_eban-banfn
                       bnfpo = lwa_eban-bnfpo.
*   Case item: no pr item found in ZTMMPRWEIGHT
              IF sy-subrc NE 0.
                "<<< CHG-24 Start comment
*                p_o_error = 'X'.
*                CLEAR lv_msg.
*                CONCATENATE lwa_eban-banfn '/' lwa_eban-bnfpo
*                INTO lv_msg.
*                MESSAGE s000(38) DISPLAY LIKE 'E'
**                WITH 'ต้องบันทึกเกณฑ์การพิจารณา'   lv_msg "lwa_eban-banfn
**                     'ให้ครบถ้วนก่อนพิมพ์ใบขอซื้อขอจ้าง'.
*                WITH 'ไม่พบข้อมูลเกณฑ์การพิจารณา'   lv_msg. "lwa_eban-banfn
**                     'ให้ครบถ้วนก่อนพิมพ์ใบขอซื้
*                EXIT.
                ">>> CHG-24 End comment
              ENDIF.

            ENDIF.
          ELSE.
*   Error: no record found in ZTMMPRWEIGHT
            "<<< CHG-24 Start comment
*            P_O_ERROR = 'X'.
*            MESSAGE S000(38) DISPLAY LIKE 'E'
*            WITH 'ไม่พบข้อมูลเกณฑ์การพิจารณา'   LWA_EBAN-BANFN.
**                 'ให้ครบถ้วนก่อนพิมพ์ใบขอซื้อขอจ้าง'.
*            EXIT.
            ">>> CHG-24 End comment
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
* Append ranges for search help: P_R1, P_R2, P_R3
      CLEAR r_ranges.
      r_ranges = 'ICP'.
      CONCATENATE '*' lwa_eban-bsart+3(1) '*'
      INTO r_ranges-low.
      APPEND r_ranges.
    ENDLOOP.
    IF p_o_error EQ 'X'.

    ELSE.
      SORT r_ranges BY low.
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM r_ranges
      COMPARING low.
      DESCRIBE TABLE r_ranges LINES lv_ranges.
      IF lv_ranges > 1.
        p_o_error = 'X'."Error
        MESSAGE s000(38) DISPLAY LIKE 'E'
        WITH 'วิธีจัดหาและเหตุผลในการจัดหาไม่ตรงกัน'
             'ไม่สามารถพิมพ์เอกสารพร้อมกันได้'.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " VALIDATE_EBAN

*&      Form  POP_UP_TO_CONFIRM
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  PF_GET_WEIGHT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*  -->  p1        text
*  <--  p2        text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_get_weight .
  DATA: lt_weight_sub  TYPE TABLE OF ztmmprweight,
        lwa_weight_sub LIKE LINE OF lt_weight_sub,
        lwa_weight     LIKE LINE OF gt_weight.
  FIELD-SYMBOLS: <lfs_weight> LIKE LINE OF gt_weight.
  IF NOT s_banfn[] IS INITIAL.
    SELECT * INTO TABLE gt_weight
    FROM ztmmprweight
    WHERE banfn IN s_banfn.
  ENDIF.
  lt_weight_sub[] = gt_weight[].
  DELETE gt_weight WHERE zweight EQ 0.
  DELETE gt_weight WHERE zparent NE 0.
  DELETE lt_weight_sub WHERE zparent IS INITIAL.
  LOOP AT lt_weight_sub INTO lwa_weight_sub.
    READ TABLE gt_weight ASSIGNING <lfs_weight>
    WITH KEY banfn = lwa_weight_sub-banfn
             bnfpo = lwa_weight_sub-bnfpo
             zseq  = lwa_weight_sub-zparent.
    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.
      <lfs_weight>-zweight = <lfs_weight>-zweight + lwa_weight_sub-zweight.
    ELSE.
      CLEAR lwa_weight.
      lwa_weight = lwa_weight_sub.
      lwa_weight-zseq = lwa_weight_sub-zparent.
      CLEAR: lwa_weight-zparent, lwa_weight-ztext_sub.
      APPEND lwa_weight TO gt_weight.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.
  DELETE gt_weight WHERE zweight EQ 0.

ENDFORM.                    " PF_GET_WEIGHT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  GET_DESC
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
*      -->P_P_R1  text
*      <--P_P_R11  text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_desc  USING    p_i_data
               CHANGING p_o_ldesc.
  DATA: lv_ldesc TYPE ztmmresult-ztextid,
        lt_lines TYPE TABLE OF tline,                             "INS CHG-25
        lv_name  TYPE thead-tdname.                             "INS CHG-25
  IF p_i_data IS INITIAL.
    CLEAR: p_o_ldesc, gt_lines_tmp.                             "ADD gt_lines_tmp CHG-25
  ELSE.

    SELECT SINGLE ztextid INTO lv_ldesc
      FROM ztmmresult
      WHERE zdata EQ p_i_data.
    MOVE lv_ldesc TO lv_name.
*<<< BOI CHG-25
    REFRESH: lt_lines, gt_lines_tmp.
    CALL FUNCTION 'READ_TEXT'
      EXPORTING
*       CLIENT                  = SY-MANDT
        id                      = 'ST'
        language                = '2'
        name                    = lv_name
        object                  = 'TEXT'
      TABLES
        lines                   = lt_lines
      EXCEPTIONS
        id                      = 1
        language                = 2
        name                    = 3
        not_found               = 4
        object                  = 5
        reference_check         = 6
        wrong_access_to_archive = 7
        OTHERS                  = 8.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      LOOP AT lt_lines INTO DATA(lw_lines).
        CONCATENATE p_o_ldesc lw_lines-tdline INTO p_o_ldesc.
      ENDLOOP.

      gt_lines_tmp[] = lt_lines[].
*>>> EOI CHG-25
*    IF sy-subrc EQ 0.                                        "DEL CHG-25
*      p_o_ldesc = lv_ldesc.                                  "DEL CHG-25
    ELSE.
      CLEAR p_o_ldesc.
    ENDIF.

  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.                    " GET_DESC

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Form  CHECK_CASE_2017
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM check_case_2017  USING    p_chk_2017.
  TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_cal,
            banfn TYPE eban-banfn,
            menge TYPE eban-menge,
            preis TYPE eban-preis,
            peinh TYPE eban-peinh,
            total TYPE eban-preis,
          END OF ty_cal.
  DATA it_cal TYPE TABLE OF ty_cal.
  DATA it_result TYPE TABLE OF ty_cal.
  DATA wa_cal TYPE ty_cal.

  DATA lv_bsart TYPE eban-bsart.

  IF s_banfn[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE bsart FROM eban INTO lv_bsart
    WHERE  loekz = ''
    AND banfn IN s_banfn[]
    AND ( bsart LIKE '%A'
      OR  bsart LIKE '%E'
      OR  bsart LIKE '%I' ).
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SELECT banfn menge preis peinh
        INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE it_cal
        FROM eban
        WHERE banfn IN s_banfn[].
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        LOOP AT it_cal INTO wa_cal.
          IF wa_cal-peinh NE 0.
            wa_cal-total = ( wa_cal-menge *  wa_cal-preis ) /  wa_cal-peinh.
          ELSE.
            wa_cal-total = 0.
          ENDIF.
          CLEAR : wa_cal-menge , wa_cal-preis , wa_cal-peinh.
          COLLECT wa_cal INTO it_result.
        ENDLOOP.
        CLEAR wa_cal.
        LOOP AT it_result INTO wa_cal.
          IF wa_cal-total <= 100000.
            p_chk_2017 = 'X'.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDLOOP.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
  ELSE.
    p_chk_2017 = ''.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.                    " CHECK_CASE_2017

FORM user_command_alv USING r_ucomm    LIKE sy-ucomm
                       rs_selfield TYPE slis_selfield.

  DATA: gd_repid LIKE sy-repid, "Exists
        ref_grid TYPE REF TO cl_gui_alv_grid. "new

*then insert the following code in your USER_COMMAND routine...

  IF ref_grid IS INITIAL.
    CALL FUNCTION 'GET_GLOBALS_FROM_SLVC_FULLSCR'
      IMPORTING
        e_grid = ref_grid.
  ENDIF.

  IF NOT ref_grid IS INITIAL.
    CALL METHOD ref_grid->check_changed_data.
  ENDIF.


  CASE r_ucomm.
    WHEN 'BACK' OR 'CANC' OR 'EXIT' OR '&F03' OR '&F15' OR '&F12'.
      LEAVE TO SCREEN 0.
    WHEN '&ADD'.
      IF rs_selfield-tabindex IS NOT INITIAL.
        READ TABLE it_ztmmpr_approve INTO DATA(lw_ztmmpr_approve) INDEX rs_selfield-tabindex.
        IF sy-subrc = 0.
*        CLEAR: lw_ztmmpr_approve-name1,
*               lw_ztmmpr_approve-name2,
*               lw_ztmmpr_approve-name3,
*               lw_ztmmpr_approve-name4,
*               lw_ztmmpr_approve-item_desc,
*               lw_ztmmpr_approve-amt.
          APPEND lw_ztmmpr_approve TO it_ztmmpr_approve.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    WHEN '&DEL'.
      DELETE it_ztmmpr_approve INDEX rs_selfield-tabindex.
    WHEN '&SAV'.
      SELECT banfn, bnfpo
        FROM eban
        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_eban)
        FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @it_ztmmpr_approve
        WHERE banfn = @it_ztmmpr_approve-banfn
          AND loekz = ' '.

      LOOP AT lt_eban INTO DATA(ls_eban)
     GROUP BY ( banfn = ls_eban-banfn ) ASCENDING ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<lfs_eban_t>).

        DATA(lt_eban_tmp) = lt_eban[].
        DATA(it_ztmmpr_approve_tmp) = it_ztmmpr_approve[].

*        LOOP AT GROUP <lfs_eban_t> ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<lfs_eban>).
        DELETE lt_eban_tmp WHERE banfn <> <lfs_eban_t>-banfn.
        DELETE it_ztmmpr_approve_tmp WHERE banfn <> <lfs_eban_t>-banfn.
        DESCRIBE TABLE lt_eban_tmp LINES DATA(lv_lines_eban).
        DESCRIBE TABLE it_ztmmpr_approve_tmp LINES DATA(lv_lines_ztmmpr).
        IF lv_lines_ztmmpr > lv_lines_eban.
          MESSAGE 'จำนวนรายการมากกว่ารายการใบขอซื้อขอจ้าง' TYPE 'E'.
        ENDIF.
*        ENDLOOP.
      ENDLOOP.

      it_ztmmpr_approve_tmp = it_ztmmpr_approve[].
*      DATA(it_ztmmpr_approve_tmp) = it_ztmmpr_approve[].
      DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM it_ztmmpr_approve_tmp COMPARING banfn.
      LOOP AT it_ztmmpr_approve_tmp INTO DATA(lw_ztmmpr_approve_tmp).
        DELETE FROM ztmmpr_approve WHERE banfn = lw_ztmmpr_approve_tmp-banfn.
      ENDLOOP.
*      DELETE FROM ztmmpr_approve WHERE banfn = gt_header-banfn.
      MODIFY ztmmpr_approve FROM TABLE it_ztmmpr_approve.
      MESSAGE 'บันทึกเรียบร้อย' TYPE 'S'.

  ENDCASE.
  rs_selfield-refresh = 'X'.

ENDFORM.  "User_command
