*<<< BOI CHG-25
FORM pf_chk_autho.

  CLEAR: gv_auth.

*--->Check authorization
  SELECT werks
    FROM eban
  INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_werks)
  WHERE banfn IN @s_banfn.
  DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_werks.

  LOOP AT lt_werks INTO DATA(ls_werks).
    AUTHORITY-CHECK OBJECT 'M_BANF_WRK'
               ID 'ACTVT' DUMMY
               ID 'WERKS' FIELD ls_werks-werks.

    IF sy-subrc <> 0.
      MESSAGE s897(sd) WITH 'คุณไม่มีสิทธิในโรงงาน' ls_werks-werks '' .
      gv_auth = 1.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

ENDFORM.

FORM get_name_position USING im_frgco TYPE ztwf_pr_log-frgco
                             im_name  TYPE pa0002-pernr
                             im_sign  TYPE ztbc_signature-esign
                             im_txt1  LIKE t13_txt1
                             im_txt2  LIKE t13_txt2
                             im_txt3  LIKE t13_txt3
                             im_txt4  LIKE t13_txt4.

  DATA: v_flag_name      TYPE boolean,
        v_flag_name_deli TYPE boolean.

  DATA: lr_fistl  TYPE RANGE OF fistl,
        lrr_fistl LIKE LINE OF lr_fistl.

*-> CHG-28(-+)
*  SELECT *
*      FROM ztwf_pr_log
*      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_ztwf_pr_log)
*        WHERE banfn = @eban-banfn
*          AND frgco = @im_frgco.

  DATA: lv_ekgrp TYPE ekgrp,
        lv_fistl TYPE fistl,
        lv_geber TYPE bp_geber.

*  SELECT SINGLE * INTO @DATA(ls_eban)           "<< CHG-32: delete
  SELECT * INTO @DATA(ls_eban)                  "<< CHG-32: insert
    UP TO 1 ROWS                                 "<< CHG-32: insert
    FROM eban
    WHERE banfn = @eban-banfn
     AND  LOEKZ = ' '                           "<< CHG-32: insert
    ORDER BY bnfpo ASCENDING.                   "<< CHG-32: insert
  ENDSELECT.                                    "<< CHG-32: insert

*  SELECT SINGLE * INTO @DATA(ls_ebkn)          "<< CHG-32: delete
  SELECT * INTO @DATA(ls_ebkn)                  "<< CHG-32: insert
    UP TO 1 ROWS                                "<< CHG-32: insert
    FROM ebkn
    WHERE banfn = @eban-banfn
     AND  LOEKZ = ' '                           "<< CHG-32: insert
    ORDER BY bnfpo ASCENDING.                   "<< CHG-32: insert
  ENDSELECT.                                    "<< CHG-32: insert

  IF ls_eban-ekgrp IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_ekgrp = ls_eban-ekgrp.
  ENDIF.

  IF ls_eban-fistl IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_fistl = ls_eban-fistl.
  ELSEIF eban-zzmm_fistl IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_fistl = ls_eban-zzmm_fistl.
  ELSE.
    lv_fistl = ls_ebkn-fistl.
  ENDIF.

  IF ls_eban-geber IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_geber = ls_eban-geber.
  ELSEIF ls_eban-zzmm_geber IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_geber = ls_eban-zzmm_geber.
  ELSE.
    lv_geber = ls_ebkn-geber.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT * UP TO 1 ROWS
    FROM ztwf_pr_log
    INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_ztwf_pr_log)
    WHERE banfn = @eban-banfn
      AND frgco = @im_frgco
    ORDER BY crdate DESCENDING, crtime DESCENDING.

*<- CHG-28(-+)

  IF sy-subrc = 0.

    SORT lt_ztwf_pr_log BY crdate DESCENDING crtime DESCENDING.
    "Case when aprc_ttxt = 01
    READ TABLE lt_ztwf_pr_log INTO DATA(lw_ztwf_pr_log) WITH KEY aprv_ttxt = '01'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.

      SELECT pernr, fistl, geber, xubname, ekgrp , pernr_owner,
        frgco, frggr    "<- CHG-28(+)
      FROM ztmm_rel_user
      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_pernr_deli)
      WHERE name_owner = @lw_ztwf_pr_log-us_name
*        AND frgco = @im_frgco                "<-CHG-28(-)
        AND xubname = @lw_ztwf_pr_log-cruser  "<-CHG-28(+)
        AND begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
        AND endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.

      SELECT pernr, fistl, geber, xubname, ekgrp , pernr_owner ,
             frgco, frggr    "<- CHG-28(+)
      FROM ztmm_rel_user
      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_pernr)
      WHERE xubname = @lw_ztwf_pr_log-cruser
          AND endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate   "<- CHG-28(+)
          AND begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.  "<- CHG-28(+)

*-> CHG-28(+)
      LOOP AT lt_pernr INTO DATA(ls_chk_pernr).
        IF NOT ( im_frgco CP ls_chk_pernr-frgco
             AND lv_ekgrp CP ls_chk_pernr-ekgrp
             AND lv_fistl CP ls_chk_pernr-fistl
             AND lv_geber CP ls_chk_pernr-geber ).
          DELETE lt_pernr INDEX sy-tabix.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      LOOP AT lt_pernr_deli INTO DATA(ls_chk_pernr_deli).
        IF NOT ( im_frgco CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-frgco
             AND eban-ekgrp CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-ekgrp
*             AND eban-fistl CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-fistl                         "CHG-34: DEL
*             AND eban-geber CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-geber ).                        "CHG-34: DEL
             AND lv_fistl CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-fistl                         "CHG-34: INS
             AND lv_geber CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-geber ).                        "CHG-34: INS
          DELETE lt_pernr_deli INDEX sy-tabix.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      READ TABLE lt_pernr TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY frggr = 'PR'.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        DELETE lt_pernr WHERE frggr <> 'PR'.
      ELSE.
        DELETE lt_pernr WHERE frggr <> 'DE'.
      ENDIF.

      READ TABLE lt_pernr_deli TRANSPORTING NO FIELDS WITH KEY frggr = 'PR'.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        DELETE lt_pernr_deli WHERE frggr <> 'PR'.
      ELSE.
        DELETE lt_pernr_deli WHERE frggr <> 'DE'.
      ENDIF.
*<- CHG-28(+)

    ELSE.

      " Normal Case
      CLEAR lw_ztwf_pr_log.
      READ TABLE lt_ztwf_pr_log INTO lw_ztwf_pr_log  INDEX 1.
      IF lw_ztwf_pr_log-us_name IS NOT INITIAL.
        SELECT pernr, fistl, geber, xubname, ekgrp , pernr_owner,
          frgco, frggr    "<- CHG-28(+)
        FROM ztmm_rel_user
        INTO TABLE @lt_pernr
        WHERE xubname = @lw_ztwf_pr_log-cruser
          AND frggr   = 'DE'                      "<-CHG-28(+)
          AND endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate     "<-CHG-28(+)
          AND begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.    "<-CHG-28(+)

        SELECT pernr, fistl, geber, xubname, ekgrp , pernr_owner,
          frgco, frggr    "<- CHG-28(+)
        FROM ztmm_rel_user
        INTO TABLE @lt_pernr_deli
        WHERE xubname = @lw_ztwf_pr_log-us_name
          AND frggr = 'PR'                      "<-CHG-28(+)
          AND endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate   "<-CHG-28(+)
          AND begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.  "<-CHG-28(+)
*        AND frgco = @im_frgco.
*        AND ( fistl = @eban-fistl
*           OR fistl = '*' )
*        AND ( geber = @eban-geber
*           OR geber = '*' ).
      ELSE.
        SELECT pernr, fistl, geber, xubname, ekgrp , pernr_owner,
          frgco, frggr    "<- CHG-28(+)
        FROM ztmm_rel_user
        INTO TABLE @lt_pernr
        WHERE xubname = @lw_ztwf_pr_log-cruser
          AND frggr = 'PR'                      "<-CHG-28(+)
          AND endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
          AND begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.
*          AND frgco = @im_frgco.               "<-CHG-28(-)

      ENDIF.

*-> CHG-28(+)
      LOOP AT lt_pernr INTO ls_chk_pernr.
        IF NOT ( im_frgco CP ls_chk_pernr-frgco
             AND lv_ekgrp CP ls_chk_pernr-ekgrp
             AND lv_fistl CP ls_chk_pernr-fistl
             AND lv_geber CP ls_chk_pernr-geber ).
          DELETE lt_pernr INDEX sy-tabix.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      LOOP AT lt_pernr_deli INTO ls_chk_pernr_deli.
        IF NOT ( im_frgco CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-frgco
             AND eban-ekgrp CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-ekgrp
             AND eban-fistl CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-fistl
             AND eban-geber CP ls_chk_pernr_deli-geber ).
          DELETE lt_pernr_deli INDEX sy-tabix.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.
*<- CHG-28(+)

    ENDIF.

    IF lw_ztwf_pr_log-us_name IS INITIAL.

      DATA(lt_tmp_pernr) = lt_pernr[].
*      DATA: lv_fistl TYPE eban-fistl,    "<-CHG-28(-)
*             lv_geber TYPE eban-geber.   "<-CHG-28(-)

      REFRESH lt_pernr[].

      IF ebkn-fistl IS NOT INITIAL.

        LOOP AT lt_tmp_pernr INTO DATA(ls_pernr).

          IF eban-ekgrp CP ls_pernr-ekgrp AND
             ebkn-fistl CP ls_pernr-fistl AND
             ebkn-geber CP ls_pernr-geber.

            APPEND ls_pernr TO lt_pernr.
            CLEAR ls_pernr.
          ENDIF.

        ENDLOOP.

      ELSE.

        LOOP AT lt_tmp_pernr INTO ls_pernr.

          IF eban-knttp = 'U'.

            lv_fistl = eban-zzmm_fistl.

            lv_geber = eban-zzmm_geber.

          ELSE.

            lv_fistl = eban-fistl.

            lv_geber = eban-geber.

          ENDIF.

          IF eban-ekgrp CP ls_pernr-ekgrp AND
             lv_fistl CP ls_pernr-fistl AND
             lv_geber CP ls_pernr-geber.

            APPEND ls_pernr TO lt_pernr.
            CLEAR ls_pernr.

          ENDIF.

        ENDLOOP.

      ENDIF.

      DATA(lt_tmp_deli) = lt_pernr_deli[].
      CLEAR: lv_fistl , lv_geber.

      REFRESH lt_pernr_deli[].

      IF ebkn-fistl IS NOT INITIAL.

        LOOP AT lt_tmp_deli INTO ls_pernr .

          IF eban-ekgrp CP ls_pernr-ekgrp AND
             ebkn-fistl CP ls_pernr-fistl AND
             ebkn-geber CP ls_pernr-geber.

            APPEND ls_pernr TO lt_pernr_deli.
            CLEAR ls_pernr.

          ENDIF.

        ENDLOOP.

      ELSE.

        LOOP AT lt_tmp_deli INTO ls_pernr.

          IF eban-knttp = 'U'.

            lv_fistl = eban-zzmm_fistl.

            lv_geber = eban-zzmm_geber.

          ELSE.

            lv_fistl = eban-fistl.

            lv_geber = eban-geber.

          ENDIF.

          IF eban-ekgrp CP ls_pernr-ekgrp AND
             lv_fistl CP ls_pernr-fistl AND
             lv_geber CP ls_pernr-geber.

            APPEND ls_pernr TO lt_pernr_deli.
            CLEAR ls_pernr.

          ENDIF.

        ENDLOOP.

      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.


    SORT lt_pernr BY fistl DESCENDING geber DESCENDING.
    CLEAR: v_flag_name.
    LOOP AT lt_pernr INTO DATA(lw_pernr).
      IF lw_ztwf_pr_log-us_name IS NOT INITIAL.
        lw_pernr-ekgrp = eban-ekgrp.
        lw_pernr-fistl = eban-fistl.
        lw_pernr-geber = eban-geber.
      ENDIF.
      IF eban-ekgrp CP lw_pernr-ekgrp AND eban-fistl CP lw_pernr-fistl AND eban-geber CP lw_pernr-geber AND v_flag_name IS INITIAL.
        v_flag_name = 'X'.
        SELECT a~pernr, a~vorna AS vorna_sig, a~nachn AS nachn_sig, a~vorsw AS vorsw_sig, a~anrlt AS anrlt_sig,
                      b~vorna AS vorna_pa, b~nachn AS nachn_pa, b~vorsw AS vorsw_pa, b~titl2 AS titl2_pa, b~knznm,
                      esign, stext, tline, c~subty,
                      c~plans AS plans_9001,
                      d~plans AS plans_0001,
                      e~anrlt AS anrlt_t522t
        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_sig_name)
        FROM ztbc_signature AS a LEFT JOIN pa0002 AS b ON a~pernr = b~pernr
                                                      AND b~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                                      AND b~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                 LEFT JOIN pa9001 AS c ON a~pernr = c~pernr
                                                      AND c~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                                      AND c~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                                      AND ( c~subty = '0001'
                                                         OR c~subty = '0002'
                                                         OR c~subty = '0003' )
                                 LEFT JOIN pa0001 AS d ON a~pernr = d~pernr
                                                      AND d~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                                      AND d~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                 LEFT JOIN t522t  AS e ON b~anred = e~anred
                                                      AND e~sprsl = '2'
        WHERE a~pernr = @lw_pernr-pernr
          AND a~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
          AND a~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.

        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          SORT lt_sig_name BY subty.
          READ TABLE lt_sig_name INTO DATA(lw_sig_name) INDEX 1.
          im_name = lw_sig_name-pernr.
          im_sign = lw_sig_name-esign.
          REFRESH: lt_sig_name.
          IF im_frgco = '13'.
            MOVE lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate TO gv_crdate.
          ENDIF.
          IF lw_sig_name-plans_9001 IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE tline
              INTO @DATA(lv_hrt1002_tline)
              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name-plans_9001 )
                AND subty = '9001'
                AND endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                AND begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.
          ELSEIF lw_sig_name-plans_0001 IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE tline
              INTO @lv_hrt1002_tline
              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name-plans_0001 )
                AND subty = '9001' "AND subty = '0001'
                AND endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                AND begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.
          ENDIF.

          IF lv_hrt1002_tline IS NOT INITIAL.
            im_txt2 = lv_hrt1002_tline.
          ELSE.
            im_txt2 = lw_sig_name-tline.
          ENDIF.

          IF lw_sig_name-knznm = '01'.
            CLEAR: gv_fullname.
            CALL FUNCTION 'ZHR_GET_TITLENAME'
              EXPORTING
                pernr     = im_name
              IMPORTING
                full_name = gv_fullname.
            MOVE gv_fullname TO im_txt1.
          ELSE.
            IF lw_sig_name-vorsw_pa IS NOT INITIAL OR lw_sig_name-titl2_pa IS NOT INITIAL.
              im_txt1 = lw_sig_name-vorsw_pa && lw_sig_name-titl2_pa.
            ELSE.
              im_txt3 = lw_sig_name-anrlt_t522t.
            ENDIF.
            IF im_txt1 IS INITIAL OR im_txt3 IS INITIAL.
              IF lw_sig_name-vorsw_sig IS NOT INITIAL.
                im_txt1 = lw_sig_name-vorsw_sig.
              ELSE.
                im_txt3 = lw_sig_name-anrlt_sig.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.

*-> Begin: CHG-28(-+)
*          IF lw_sig_name-vorna_sig IS NOT INITIAL AND lw_sig_name-nachn_sig IS NOT INITIAL.
*            im_txt3 = im_txt3 && lw_sig_name-vorna_sig.
*            im_txt4 = lw_sig_name-nachn_sig.
*          ELSE.
*            im_txt3 = im_txt3 && lw_sig_name-vorna_pa.
*            im_txt4 = lw_sig_name-nachn_pa.
*          ENDIF.

          IF lw_sig_name-vorna_pa IS NOT INITIAL AND lw_sig_name-nachn_pa IS NOT INITIAL.
            im_txt3 = im_txt3 && lw_sig_name-vorna_pa.
            im_txt4 = lw_sig_name-nachn_pa.
          ELSE.
            im_txt3 = im_txt3 && lw_sig_name-vorna_sig.
            im_txt4 = lw_sig_name-nachn_sig.
          ENDIF.
*<- End: CHG-28(-+)
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.


    SORT lt_pernr_deli BY fistl DESCENDING geber DESCENDING.
    DATA: lv_posi_deli TYPE string.
    CLEAR: v_flag_name_deli.
    LOOP AT lt_pernr_deli INTO DATA(lw_pernr_deli).

      IF lv_posi_deli IS INITIAL.

        CLEAR v_flag_name_deli.

      ENDIF.


      READ TABLE lt_ztwf_pr_log INTO DATA(ls_pr_log) WITH KEY aprv_ttxt = '01'.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.

        lw_pernr_deli-pernr = lw_pernr_deli-pernr_owner.

      ENDIF.
*      IF eban-ekgrp CP lw_pernr_deli-ekgrp AND eban-fistl CP lw_pernr_deli-fistl AND eban-geber CP lw_pernr_deli-geber               "CHG-34: DEL
      IF eban-ekgrp CP lw_pernr_deli-ekgrp AND lv_fistl CP lw_pernr_deli-fistl AND lv_geber CP lw_pernr_deli-geber               "CHG-34: INS
        AND v_flag_name_deli IS INITIAL.
        v_flag_name_deli = 'X'.
        SELECT a~pernr, a~vorna AS vorna_sig, a~nachn AS nachn_sig, a~vorsw AS vorsw_sig, a~anrlt AS anrlt_sig,
                      b~vorna AS vorna_pa, b~nachn AS nachn_pa, b~vorsw AS vorsw_pa, b~titl2 AS titl2_pa,
                      esign, stext, tline, c~subty,
                      c~plans AS plans_9001,
                      d~plans AS plans_0001,
                      e~anrlt AS anrlt_t522t
        INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_sig_name_deli)
        FROM ztbc_signature AS a LEFT JOIN pa0002 AS b ON a~pernr = b~pernr
                                                      AND b~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                                      AND b~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                 LEFT JOIN pa9001 AS c ON a~pernr = c~pernr
                                                      AND c~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                                      AND c~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                                      AND ( c~subty = '0001'
                                                         OR c~subty = '0002'
                                                         OR c~subty = '0003' )
                                 LEFT JOIN pa0001 AS d ON a~pernr = d~pernr
                                                      AND d~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                                      AND d~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                                 LEFT JOIN t522t  AS e ON b~anred = e~anred
                                                      AND e~sprsl = '2'
        WHERE a~pernr = @lw_pernr_deli-pernr
          AND a~begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
          AND a~endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.

        IF sy-subrc = 0.
          SORT lt_sig_name_deli BY subty.
          READ TABLE lt_sig_name_deli INTO DATA(lw_sig_name_deli) INDEX 1.
          REFRESH: lt_sig_name_deli.
          IF lw_sig_name_deli-plans_9001 IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE tline
              INTO @DATA(lv_hrt1002_tline_deli)
              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name_deli-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name_deli-plans_9001 )
                AND subty = '9001'
                AND endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                AND begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.
          ELSEIF lw_sig_name_deli-plans_0001 IS NOT INITIAL.
            SELECT SINGLE tline
              INTO @lv_hrt1002_tline_deli
              FROM hrp1002 AS a INNER JOIN hrt1002 AS b ON a~tabnr = b~tabnr
              WHERE ( objid = @lw_sig_name_deli-pernr
                   OR objid = @lw_sig_name_deli-plans_0001 )
                   AND subty = '9001' "AND subty = '0001'
                AND endda >= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate
                AND begda <= @lw_ztwf_pr_log-crdate.
          ENDIF.

          IF lv_hrt1002_tline_deli IS NOT INITIAL.
            lv_posi_deli = lv_hrt1002_tline_deli.
          ELSE.
            lv_posi_deli = lw_sig_name_deli-tline.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

  IF lw_ztwf_pr_log-aprv_ttxt IS NOT INITIAL AND im_txt2 IS NOT INITIAL.
    CASE lw_ztwf_pr_log-aprv_ttxt.
      WHEN '01'.
        CONCATENATE im_txt2 'ปฏิบัติการแทน' lv_posi_deli INTO im_txt2 SEPARATED BY space.
      WHEN '02'.
        CONCATENATE im_txt2 'รักษาการแทน' lv_posi_deli INTO im_txt2 SEPARATED BY space.
    ENDCASE.
  ENDIF.

  CLEAR: lw_ztwf_pr_log, lw_sig_name, lt_pernr[], lt_sig_name[].

ENDFORM.

*>>> EOI CHG-25

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      Module  GET_F4_REASON  INPUT
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*       text
*----------------------------------------------------------------------*
MODULE get_f4_reason INPUT.

  PERFORM get_f4_reason CHANGING p_r1.

ENDMODULE.

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form GET_F4_REASON
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      <-- P_R1
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM get_f4_reason  CHANGING pv_r1.
  DATA: lv_field TYPE dfies-fieldname.
  DATA: lt_ret   TYPE TABLE OF ddshretval.
  DATA: lr_bsart TYPE RANGE OF bsart.

  IF s_banfn IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT SINGLE bsart
      INTO @DATA(lv_bsart)
      FROM eban
      WHERE banfn IN @s_banfn
        AND loekz EQ @space.
    IF sy-subrc IS INITIAL.
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO lr_bsart
             ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs_bsart>).
      <fs_bsart>-sign   = 'I'.
      <fs_bsart>-option = 'EQ'.
      <fs_bsart>-low    = lv_bsart.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT zdata, zdesc, zldesc
    FROM ztmmresult
    INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_data)
    WHERE zpr_method_range IN @lr_bsart.

  CALL FUNCTION 'F4IF_INT_TABLE_VALUE_REQUEST'
    EXPORTING
      retfield   = 'ZDATA'
      value_org  = 'S'
    TABLES
      value_tab  = lt_data
      return_tab = lt_ret.

  CHECK lt_ret[] IS NOT INITIAL.

  pv_r1 = lt_ret[ 1 ]-fieldval.

ENDFORM.

FORM get_pernr USING im_pa0002 LIKE  gt_pa0002
                     im_pa0002_disp LIKE gt_pa0002_disp
                     im_badat TYPE eban-badat.

  DATA: lv_badat TYPE sy-datum.

  IF im_badat IS NOT INITIAL.
    lv_badat = im_badat.
  ELSE.
    lv_badat = sy-datum.
  ENDIF.

  SELECT a~pernr, b~vorna, b~nachn, b~knznm
  INTO TABLE @im_pa0002
  FROM pa0000 AS a INNER JOIN pa0002 AS b
    ON a~pernr = b~pernr
   AND a~subty = b~subty
   AND a~objps = b~objps
   AND a~sprps = b~sprps
 WHERE ( a~begda <= @lv_badat AND a~endda >= @lv_badat ) AND
       ( b~begda <= @lv_badat AND b~endda >= @lv_badat ) AND
*           a~pernr = p_pernr AND
       ( ( a~pernr LIKE '8%' AND
           ( a~stat2 = '1' OR a~stat2 = '3' ) ) OR
         ( a~pernr NOT LIKE '8%' AND a~stat2 = '3' ) ).

  SELECT a~pernr, b~vorna, b~nachn
      INTO TABLE @im_pa0002_disp
      FROM pa0000 AS a INNER JOIN pa0002 AS b
        ON a~pernr = b~pernr
       AND a~subty = b~subty
       AND a~objps = b~objps
       AND a~sprps = b~sprps
     WHERE ( a~begda <= @lv_badat AND a~endda >= @lv_badat ) AND
           ( b~begda <= @lv_badat AND b~endda >= @lv_badat ) AND
*           a~pernr = p_pernr AND
           ( ( a~pernr LIKE '8%' AND
               ( a~stat2 = '1' OR a~stat2 = '3' ) ) OR
             ( a~pernr NOT LIKE '8%' AND a~stat2 = '3' ) ).

ENDFORM.

*-> Begin: CHG-30(+)
FORM get_pr_log_date.

  SELECT DISTINCT banfn, bnfpo, frgzu, ekgrp
    FROM eban
    INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_frgzu)
    WHERE banfn IN @s_banfn
      AND loekz = ' '.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.

    SORT lt_frgzu BY banfn.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_frgzu COMPARING banfn.

    SELECT * FROM ztwf_pr_log
      INTO TABLE @DATA(lt_ztwf_pr_log_date)
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_frgzu
      WHERE banfn = @lt_frgzu-banfn
        AND frgco = '13'.

    LOOP AT lt_frgzu INTO DATA(ls_frgzu).
      APPEND INITIAL LINE TO gt_docs_data ASSIGNING FIELD-SYMBOL(<fs>).
      MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_frgzu TO <fs>.
      CLEAR <fs>-crdate.

      LOOP AT lt_ztwf_pr_log_date INTO DATA(ls_pr_date) WHERE banfn = <fs>-banfn.
        CHECK ls_pr_date-crdate > <fs>-crdate.
        MOVE-CORRESPONDING ls_pr_date TO <fs>.
      ENDLOOP.
    ENDLOOP.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.
*<- End: CHG-30(+)
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form VALIDATE_BSART_COMMITTEE
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*&      <-- V_ERROR
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM VALIDATE_BSART_COMMITTEE  CHANGING P_V_ERROR.
  DATA lv_result TYPE string.
  IF s_banfn[] IS NOT INITIAL.
    SELECT banfn, bsart
    INTO TABLE @data(lt_eban)
    FROM eban
    WHERE banfn IN @s_banfn
    AND   loekz EQ @space
    AND   bsart EQ '1INT'.

    SORT lt_eban BY banfn.
    DELETE ADJACENT DUPLICATES FROM lt_eban COMPARING banfn.
    IF lt_eban[] IS NOT INITIAL.

      SELECT * INTO TABLE @data(lt_commit)
      FROM ztmmprcommittee
      FOR ALL ENTRIES IN @lt_eban
      WHERE banfn EQ @lt_eban-banfn
        AND ( committee_group EQ '20'
           OR committee_group EQ '19' ).
      SORT lt_commit BY banfn committee_group.

      CLEAR lv_result.
      LOOP AT lt_eban INTO DATA(wa_eban).
        READ TABLE lt_commit INTO DATA(wa_commit) WITH KEY banfn = wa_eban-banfn
                                                 committee_group = '19'.
        IF sy-subrc NE 0.
          CONCATENATE lv_result wa_eban-banfn INTO lv_result.
          EXIT.
        ELSE.
          CLEAR wa_commit.
          READ TABLE lt_commit INTO wa_commit WITH KEY banfn = wa_eban-banfn
                                             committee_group = '20'.
          IF sy-subrc NE 0.
            CONCATENATE lv_result wa_eban-banfn INTO lv_result.
            EXIT.
          ENDIF.
        ENDIF.
      ENDLOOP.

      IF lv_result IS NOT INITIAL.
        MESSAGE s108(zcu) WITH lv_result DISPLAY LIKE 'E'.
        P_V_ERROR = 'X'.
      ENDIF.

    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDFORM.

* >>> +CHG-33

*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& Form PF_GET_QRCODE_URL
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
*& text
*&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
FORM pf_get_qrcode_url  USING    iv_banfn  TYPE eban-banfn
                        CHANGING cv_qrcode TYPE string.

  cv_qrcode = zcl_tcbc_param_2=>get_value( im_module     = 'MM'
                                           im_param_id   = 'ZLPUEN111'
                                           im_param_name = 'QRCODE_URL_PR'
                                           im_param_ext  = space
                                           im_begda      = sy-datum
                                           im_endda      = sy-datum ).

  IF cv_qrcode IS NOT INITIAL.
    REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '&1' IN cv_qrcode WITH iv_banfn.
  ENDIF.

ENDFORM.

* <<< +CHG-33
